(1-\sqrt{\epsilon})\frac{\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{F^{\prime}_{\alpha}}}{|F^{\prime}_{\alpha}|}&\leq&\alpha^{X}\;\leq\;(1+\sqrt{\epsilon})\frac{\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{F^{\prime}_{\alpha}}}{|F^{\prime}_{\alpha}|},\\
(1-\sqrt{\epsilon})\frac{\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{F^{\prime}_{\beta}}}{|F^{\prime}_{\beta}|}&\leq&\beta^{Y}\;\leq\;(1+\sqrt{\epsilon})\frac{\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{F^{\prime}_{\beta}}}{|F^{\prime}_{\beta}|},\\
(1-\sqrt{\epsilon})\frac{\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{F^{\prime}_{\rho}}}{|F^{\prime}_{\rho}|}&\leq&\rho^{M}\;\leq\;(1+\sqrt{\epsilon})\frac{\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{F^{\prime}_{\rho}}}{|F^{\prime}_{\rho}|},\\
(1-\sqrt{\epsilon})\frac{(\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{F^{\prime}_{\alpha}})^{\otimes A}\otimes(\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{F^{\prime}_{\beta}})^{\otimes B}\otimes(\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{F^{\prime}_{\rho}})}{|F^{\prime}_{\alpha}|^{A}|F^{\prime}_{\beta}|^{B}|F^{\prime}_{\rho}|}&\leq&\tau^{X^{A}Y^{B}M}\;\leq\;(1+\sqrt{\epsilon})\frac{(\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{F^{\prime}_{\alpha}})^{\otimes A}\otimes(\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{F^{\prime}_{\beta}})^{\otimes B}\otimes(\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{F^{\prime}_{\rho}})}{|F^{\prime}_{\alpha}|^{A}|F^{\prime}_{\beta}|^{B}|F^{\prime}_{\rho}|}.\end{array}

(16)

In particular,

\mathrm{supp}(\rho^{XYM})\leq F^{\prime}_{\alpha}\otimes F^{\prime}_{\beta}\otimes F^{\prime}_{\rho},~~\mathrm{supp}(\sigma^{X^{A}Y^{B}M})\leq(F^{\prime}_{\alpha})^{\otimes A}\otimes(F^{\prime}_{\beta})^{\otimes B}\otimes F^{\prime}_{\rho}=\mathrm{supp}(\tau^{X^{A}Y^{B}M}).

To finish the proof of Lemma
7
, we
only need to show

\|\sigma^{X^{A}Y^{B}M}-\tau^{X^{A}Y^{B}M}\|_{1}\leq 20\epsilon^{1/32},

(17)

because of Lemma
8
and
Fact
2
.

In order to prove the above inequality, we augment
X
to
L_{X}X
,

Y
to
L_{Y}Y
as in Section
3.4
. Note that

L_{X}
,
L_{Y}
are the
additional Hilbert spaces required for augmentation and have nothing
to do the
L_{X}
,
L_{Y}
used for flattening earlier. In fact, the
current definition of the Hilbert space
X
is actually the old
X

tensored with the old flattening
L_{X}
; same for the current definition
of
Y
. Recall that the new spaces
L_{X}
,
L_{Y}
required for augmentation
have the same dimension
L
. The value of
L
will be chosen later,
and it will be sufficiently large for our later purposes. Keeping
augmentation in mind, we define

\alpha^{L_{X}X}:=\frac{\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{L_{X}}}{L}\otimes\alpha^{X},~\beta^{L_{Y}Y}:=\frac{\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{L_{Y}}}{L}\otimes\beta^{Y},~~\rho^{L_{X}XL_{Y}YM}:=\frac{\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{L_{X}}}{L}\otimes\frac{\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{L_{Y}}}{L}\otimes\rho^{XYM}.

Then, the
A
-fold tensor product state
\alpha^{(L_{X}X)^{A}}
and
the
(|A|-1)
-fold tensor product state
\alpha^{(L_{X}X)^{-a}}
for
any
a\in[A]
can be defined as before in the natural fashion.
A similar comment holds for
\beta^{(L_{Y}Y)^{B}}
and

\beta^{(L_{Y}Y)^{-b}}
for any
b\in[B]
.

As in Section
3.4
, we enlarge the
Hilbert space
M
into a larger space
\hat{M}
. Because of this containment,
the state

\rho^{L_{X}XL_{Y}Y\hat{M}}
is identical to
\rho^{L_{X}XL_{Y}YM}
; which
notation we use depends on the application. Define the normalised
state
\hat{\rho}^{L_{X}XL_{Y}Y\hat{M}}
and subnormalised state

\hat{\hat{\rho}}^{L_{X}XL_{Y}Y\hat{M}}
as in Equation
9
via
the projectors
\Pi^{XYM}(3)
,

\Pi^{XYM}(2):=\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{X}\otimes\Pi^{YM}(2)
,

\Pi^{XYM}(1):=\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{Y}\otimes\Pi^{XM}(1)
.
Define

\begin{array}[]{rcl}\sigma^{(L_{X}X)^{A}(L_{Y}Y)^{B}\hat{M}}&:=&\sigma^{(L_{X}X)^{A}(L_{Y}Y)^{B}M}\\
&=&(AB)^{-1}\sum_{a=1}^{A}\sum_{b=1}^{B}\rho^{(L_{X}X)_{a}(L_{Y}Y)_{b}M}\otimes\alpha^{(L_{X}X)^{-a}}\otimes\beta^{(L_{Y}Y)^{-b}},\\
\hat{\sigma}^{(L_{X}X)^{A}(L_{Y}Y)^{B}\hat{M}}&:=&(AB)^{-1}\sum_{a=1}^{A}\sum_{b=1}^{B}\hat{\rho}^{(L_{X}X)_{a}(L_{Y}Y)_{b}\hat{M}}\otimes\alpha^{(L_{X}X)^{-a}}\otimes\beta^{(L_{Y}Y)^{-b}},\\
\hat{\hat{\sigma}}^{(L_{X}X)^{A}(L_{Y}Y)^{B}\hat{M}}&:=&(AB)^{-1}\sum_{a=1}^{A}\sum_{b=1}^{B}\hat{\hat{\rho}}^{(L_{X}X)_{a}(L_{Y}Y)_{b}\hat{M}}\otimes\alpha^{(L_{X}X)^{-a}}\otimes\beta^{(L_{Y}Y)^{-b}},\\
\tau^{(L_{X}X)^{A}(L_{Y}Y)^{B}\hat{M}}&:=&\tau^{(L_{X}X)^{A}(L_{Y}Y)^{B}M}\;=\;\alpha^{(L_{X}X)^{A}}\otimes\beta^{(L_{Y}Y)^{B}}\otimes\rho^{L_{M}M}.\end{array}

(18)

Observe that

\displaystyle\|\sigma^{(L_{X}X)^{A}(L_{Y}Y)^{B}\hat{M}}-\tau^{(L_{X}X)^{A}(L_{Y}Y)^{B}\hat{M}}\|_{1}

\displaystyle\equiv

\displaystyle\|\sigma^{(L_{X}X)^{A}(L_{Y}Y)^{B}M}-\tau^{(L_{X}X)^{A}(L_{Y}Y)^{B}M}\|_{1}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\|\sigma^{X^{A}Y^{B}M}-\tau^{X^{A}Y^{B}M}\|_{1}.

Note that
\sigma^{(L_{X}X)^{A}(L_{Y}Y)^{B}\hat{M}}
,

\tau^{(L_{X}X)^{A}(L_{Y}Y)^{B}\hat{M}}
,

\hat{\sigma}^{(L_{X}X)^{A}(L_{Y}Y)^{B}\hat{M}}
are normalised states and

\hat{\hat{\sigma}}^{(L_{X}X)^{A}(L_{Y}Y)^{B}\hat{M}}
is subnormalised.
So to prove Lemma
7
, we just need to
show that

\|\sigma^{(L_{X}X)^{A}(L_{Y}Y)^{B}\hat{M}}-\tau^{(L_{X}X)^{A}(L_{Y}Y)^{B}\hat{M}}\|_{1}\leq 17\epsilon^{1/32},

(19)

because of Equations
17
,
18
.

Define the tensor product spaces

L_{X}\otimes F^{\prime}_{\alpha}=:L_{X}F^{\prime}_{\alpha}\leq L_{X}X,~~L_{Y}\otimes F^{\prime}_{\beta}=:L_{Y}F^{\prime}_{\beta}\leq L_{Y}Y.

Observe that

\begin{array}[]{rcl}(1-\sqrt{\epsilon})\frac{(\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{L_{X}F^{\prime}_{\alpha}})^{-a}\otimes(\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{L_{Y}F^{\prime}_{\beta}})^{-b}}{(L|F^{\prime}_{\alpha}|)^{A-1}(L|F^{\prime}_{\beta}|)^{B-1}}&\leq&\alpha^{X^{-a}}\otimes\beta^{Y^{-b}}\\
&\leq&(1+\sqrt{\epsilon})\frac{(\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{L_{X}F^{\prime}_{\alpha}})^{-a}\otimes(\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{L_{Y}F^{\prime}_{\beta}})^{-b}}{(L|F^{\prime}_{\alpha}|)^{A-1}(L|F^{\prime}_{\beta}|)^{B-1}},\\
(1-\sqrt{\epsilon})\frac{(\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{L_{X}F^{\prime}_{\alpha}})^{\otimes A}\otimes(\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{L_{Y}F^{\prime}_{\beta}})^{\otimes B}\otimes(\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{F^{\prime}_{\rho}})}{(L|F^{\prime}_{\alpha}|)^{A}(L|F^{\prime}_{\beta}|)^{B}|F^{\prime}_{\rho}|}&\leq&\tau^{(L_{X}X)^{A}(L_{Y}Y)^{B}\hat{M}}\\
&\leq&(1+\sqrt{\epsilon})\frac{(\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{L_{X}F^{\prime}_{\alpha}})^{\otimes A}\otimes(\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{L_{Y}F^{\prime}_{\beta}})^{\otimes B}(\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{F^{\prime}_{\rho}})}{(L|F^{\prime}_{\alpha}|)^{A}(L|F^{\prime}_{\beta}|)^{B}|F^{\prime}_{\rho}|},\end{array}

(20)

for any
a\in[A]
,
b\in[B]
.

We now prove four lemmas which are crucially required in the following
arguments, leading to the proof of
Lemma
7
.

Lemma 9

\Tr[\hat{\rho}^{L_{X}XL_{Y}Y\hat{M}}\hat{\hat{\rho}}^{L_{X}XL_{Y}Y\hat{M}}]\leq\frac{(1+3\sqrt{\epsilon})2^{D^{\epsilon}_{\infty}(\rho^{XYM}\|\alpha^{X}\otimes\beta^{Y}\otimes\rho^{M})}}{(L|F^{\prime}_{\alpha}|)(LF^{\prime}_{\beta})|F^{\prime}_{\rho}|}.

Proof:

Using Equations
9
,
8
,

16
, we get

\displaystyle\Tr[\hat{\rho}^{L_{X}XL_{Y}Y\hat{M}}\hat{\hat{\rho}}^{L_{X}XL_{Y}Y\hat{M}}]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Tr[\hat{\rho}^{L_{X}XL_{Y}Y\hat{M}}(\hat{\Pi}^{L_{X}XL_{Y}Y\hat{M}}\circ\rho^{L_{X}XL_{Y}Y\hat{M}})]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Tr[T^{L_{X}XL_{Y}YM\rightarrow L_{X}XL_{Y}Y\hat{M}}_{\epsilon^{1/4}}(\rho^{L_{X}XL_{Y}YM})

\displaystyle~~~~~~~~((\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{L_{X}XL_{Y}Y\hat{M}}-T^{L_{X}XL_{Y}YM\rightarrow L_{X}XL_{Y}Y\hat{M}}_{\epsilon^{1/4}}(\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{L_{X}L_{Y}}\otimes(\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{XYM}-\Pi^{XYM}(3))))

\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~{}\circ(\hat{\Pi}^{L_{X}XL_{Y}Y\hat{M}}\circ\rho^{L_{X}XL_{Y}Y\hat{M}}))]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Tr[(T^{L_{X}XL_{Y}YM\rightarrow L_{X}XL_{Y}Y\hat{M}}_{\epsilon^{1/4}}(\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{L_{X}XL_{Y}YM})\circ T^{L_{X}XL_{Y}YM\rightarrow L_{X}XL_{Y}Y\hat{M}}_{\epsilon^{1/4}}(\rho^{L_{X}XL_{Y}YM}))

\displaystyle~~~~~~~~((\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{L_{X}XL_{Y}Y\hat{M}}-T^{L_{X}XL_{Y}YM\rightarrow L_{X}XL_{Y}Y\hat{M}}_{\epsilon^{1/4}}(\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{L_{X}L_{Y}}\otimes(\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{XYM}-\Pi^{XYM}(3))))

\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~{}\circ\hat{\hat{\rho}}^{L_{X}XL_{Y}Y\hat{M}})]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Tr[T^{L_{X}XL_{Y}YM\rightarrow L_{X}XL_{Y}Y\hat{M}}_{\epsilon^{1/4}}(\rho^{L_{X}XL_{Y}YM})

\displaystyle~~~~~~~~(T^{L_{X}XL_{Y}YM\rightarrow L_{X}XL_{Y}Y\hat{M}}_{\epsilon^{1/4}}(\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{L_{X}XL_{Y}YM})-{}

\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~T^{L_{X}XL_{Y}YM\rightarrow L_{X}XL_{Y}Y\hat{M}}_{\epsilon^{1/4}}(\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{L_{X}L_{Y}}\otimes(\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{XYM}-\Pi^{XYM}(3))))\circ\hat{\hat{\rho}}^{L_{X}XL_{Y}Y\hat{M}})]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Tr[T^{L_{X}XL_{Y}YM\rightarrow L_{X}XL_{Y}Y\hat{M}}_{\epsilon^{1/4}}((\frac{\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{L_{X}L_{Y}}}{L^{2}})\otimes\rho^{XYM})

\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~~(T^{L_{X}XL_{Y}YM\rightarrow L_{X}XL_{Y}Y\hat{M}}_{\epsilon^{1/4}}(\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{L_{X}L_{Y}}\otimes\Pi^{XYM}(3))\circ\hat{\hat{\rho}}^{L_{X}XL_{Y}Y\hat{M}})]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Tr[T^{L_{X}XL_{Y}YM\rightarrow L_{X}XL_{Y}Y\hat{M}}_{\epsilon^{1/4}}((\frac{\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{L_{X}L_{Y}}}{L^{2}})\otimes(\Pi^{XYM}(3)\circ\rho^{XYM}))\hat{\hat{\rho}}^{L_{X}XL_{Y}Y\hat{M}})]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|T^{L_{X}XL_{Y}YM\rightarrow L_{X}XL_{Y}Y\hat{M}}_{\epsilon^{1/4}}((\frac{\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{L_{X}L_{Y}}}{L^{2}})\otimes(\Pi^{XYM}(3)\circ\rho^{XYM}))\|_{\infty}\cdot\|\hat{\hat{\rho}}^{L_{X}XL_{Y}Y\hat{M}}\|_{1}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\|(\frac{\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{L_{X}L_{Y}}}{L^{2}})\otimes(\Pi^{XYM}(3)\circ\rho^{XYM})\|_{\infty}\cdot\|\hat{\hat{\rho}}^{L_{X}XL_{Y}Y\hat{M}}\|_{1}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle L^{-2}\cdot\|\Pi^{XYM}(3)\circ\rho^{XYM}\|_{\infty}\cdot\|\hat{\hat{\rho}}^{L_{X}XL_{Y}Y\hat{M}}\|_{1}\;\leq\;\frac{(1+3\sqrt{\epsilon})2^{D^{\epsilon}_{\infty}(\rho^{XYM}\|\alpha^{X}\otimes\beta^{Y}\otimes\rho^{M})}}{(L|F^{\prime}_{\alpha}|)(L|F^{\prime}_{\beta}|)|F^{\prime}_{\rho}|}.

Above, we used the fact that

\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{L_{X}XL_{Y}Y\hat{M}}-T^{L_{X}XL_{Y}YM\rightarrow L_{X}XL_{Y}Y\hat{M}}_{\epsilon^{1/4}}(\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{L_{X}L_{Y}}\otimes(\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{XYM}-\Pi^{XYM}(3)))\geq\hat{\Pi}^{L_{X}XL_{Y}Y\hat{M}}

in the second equality,

\displaystyle(\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{L_{X}XL_{Y}Y\hat{M}}-T^{L_{X}XL_{Y}YM\rightarrow L_{X}XL_{Y}Y\hat{M}}_{\epsilon^{1/4}}(\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{L_{X}L_{Y}}\otimes(\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{XYM}-\Pi^{XYM}(3))))

\displaystyle~~~~~~(T^{L_{X}XL_{Y}YM\rightarrow L_{X}XL_{Y}Y\hat{M}}_{\epsilon^{1/4}}(\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{L_{X}XL_{Y}YM}))

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle T^{L_{X}XL_{Y}YM\rightarrow L_{X}XL_{Y}Y\hat{M}}_{\epsilon^{1/4}}(\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{L_{X}XL_{Y}YM})

\displaystyle~~~{}-T^{L_{X}XL_{Y}YM\rightarrow L_{X}XL_{Y}Y\hat{M}}_{\epsilon^{1/4}}(\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{L_{X}L_{Y}}\otimes(\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{XYM}-\Pi^{XYM}(3)))

in the fourth equality,

T^{L_{X}XL_{Y}YM\rightarrow L_{X}XL_{Y}Y\hat{M}}_{\epsilon^{1/4}}
is
an isometry in the sixth and seventh equalities.
The proof of the lemma is now complete.

\square

Lemma 10

\Tr[\hat{\rho}^{L_{X}X\hat{M}}\hat{\hat{\rho}}^{L_{X}X\hat{M}}]\leq\frac{4\epsilon^{1/8}}{L^{3/2}}+\frac{(1+3\sqrt{\epsilon})2^{D^{\epsilon}_{\infty}(\rho^{XM}\|\alpha^{X}\otimes\rho^{M})}}{(L|F^{\prime}_{\alpha}|)|F^{\prime}_{\rho}|}.

Proof:

Using Equations
9
,
8
,

16
and Lemma
4
, we get

\displaystyle\Tr[\hat{\rho}^{L_{X}X\hat{M}}\hat{\hat{\rho}}^{L_{X}X\hat{M}}]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Tr[(\hat{\rho}^{L_{X}X\hat{M}}\otimes\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{L_{Y}Y})\hat{\hat{\rho}}^{L_{X}XL_{Y}Y\hat{M}}]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Tr[((T^{L_{X}XM\rightarrow L_{X}X\hat{M}}_{\epsilon^{1/4}}(\rho^{L_{X}XM}))\otimes\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{L_{Y}Y})\hat{\hat{\rho}}^{L_{X}XL_{Y}Y\hat{M}}]

\displaystyle~~~{}+\Tr[((\hat{\rho}^{L_{X}X\hat{M}}-T^{L_{X}XM\rightarrow L_{X}X\hat{M}}_{\epsilon^{1/4}}(\rho^{L_{X}XM}))\otimes\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{L_{Y}Y})\hat{\hat{\rho}}^{L_{X}XL_{Y}Y\hat{M}}]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Tr[((T^{L_{X}XM\rightarrow L_{X}X\hat{M}}_{\epsilon^{1/4}}(\rho^{L_{X}XM}))\otimes\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{L_{Y}Y})

\displaystyle~~~~~~~~((\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{L_{X}XL_{Y}Y\hat{M}}-(T^{L_{X}XM\rightarrow L_{X}X\hat{M}}_{\epsilon^{1/4}}\otimes\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{L_{Y}Y})(\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{L_{X}L_{Y}}\otimes(\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{XYM}-\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{Y}\otimes\Pi^{XM}(1))))

\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~{}\circ(\hat{\Pi}^{L_{X}XL_{Y}Y\hat{M}}\circ\rho^{L_{X}XL_{Y}Y\hat{M}}))]

\displaystyle~~~{}+\Tr[(\hat{\rho}^{L_{X}X\hat{M}}-T^{L_{X}XM\rightarrow L_{X}X\hat{M}}_{\epsilon^{1/4}}(\rho^{L_{X}XM}))\hat{\hat{\rho}}^{L_{X}X\hat{M}}]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Tr[(((T^{L_{X}XM\rightarrow L_{X}X\hat{M}}_{\epsilon^{1/4}}(\rho^{L_{X}XM}))

\displaystyle~~~~~~~~(\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{L_{X}X\hat{M}}-T^{L_{X}XM\rightarrow L_{X}X\hat{M}}_{\epsilon^{1/4}}(\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{L_{X}}\otimes(\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{XM}-\Pi^{XM}(1)))))\otimes\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{L_{Y}Y})\circ\hat{\hat{\rho}}^{L_{X}XL_{Y}Y\hat{M}}]

\displaystyle~~~{}+\Tr[(\hat{\rho}^{L_{X}X\hat{M}}-T^{L_{X}XM\rightarrow L_{X}X\hat{M}}_{\epsilon^{1/4}}(\rho^{L_{X}XM}))\hat{\hat{\rho}}^{L_{X}X\hat{M}}]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Tr[((T^{L_{X}XM\rightarrow L_{X}X\hat{M}}_{\epsilon^{1/4}}(\rho^{L_{X}XM}))

\displaystyle~~~~~~~~(\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{L_{X}X\hat{M}}-T^{L_{X}XM\rightarrow L_{X}X\hat{M}}_{\epsilon^{1/4}}(\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{L_{X}}\otimes(\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{XM}-\Pi^{XM}(1)))))\circ\hat{\hat{\rho}}^{L_{X}X\hat{M}}]

\displaystyle~~~{}+\Tr[(\hat{\rho}^{L_{X}X\hat{M}}-T^{L_{X}XM\rightarrow L_{X}X\hat{M}}_{\epsilon^{1/4}}(\rho^{L_{X}XM}))\hat{\hat{\rho}}^{L_{X}X\hat{M}}]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\Tr[((T^{L_{X}XM\rightarrow L_{X}X\hat{M}}_{\epsilon^{1/4}}(\rho^{L_{X}XM}))

\displaystyle~~~~~~~~(\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{L_{X}X\hat{M}}-T^{L_{X}XM\rightarrow L_{X}X\hat{M}}_{\epsilon^{1/4}}(\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{L_{X}}\otimes(\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{XM}-\Pi^{XM}(1)))))\circ\hat{\hat{\rho}}^{L_{X}X\hat{M}}]

\displaystyle~~~{}+\|\hat{\rho}^{L_{X}X\hat{M}}-T^{L_{X}XM\rightarrow L_{X}X\hat{M}}_{\epsilon^{1/4}}(\rho^{L_{X}XM})\|_{\infty}\cdot\|\hat{\hat{\rho}}^{L_{X}X\hat{M}}\|_{1}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\Tr[((T^{L_{X}XM\rightarrow L_{X}X\hat{M}}_{\epsilon^{1/4}}(\rho^{L_{X}XM}))

\displaystyle~~~~~~~~(\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{L_{X}X\hat{M}}-T^{L_{X}XM\rightarrow L_{X}X\hat{M}}_{\epsilon^{1/4}}(\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{L_{X}}\otimes(\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{XM}-\Pi^{XM}(1)))))\circ\hat{\hat{\rho}}^{L_{X}X\hat{M}}]+4\epsilon^{1/8}L^{-3/2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Tr[((T^{L_{X}XM\rightarrow L_{X}X\hat{M}}_{\epsilon^{1/4}}(\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{L_{X}XM})\circ T^{L_{X}XM\rightarrow L_{X}X\hat{M}}_{\epsilon^{1/4}}(\rho^{L_{X}XM}))

\displaystyle~~~~~~~~(\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{L_{X}X\hat{M}}-T^{L_{X}XM\rightarrow L_{X}X\hat{M}}_{\epsilon^{1/4}}(\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{L_{X}}\otimes(\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{XM}-\Pi^{XM}(1)))))\circ\hat{\hat{\rho}}^{L_{X}X\hat{M}}]+4\epsilon^{1/8}L^{-3/2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Tr[T^{L_{X}XM\rightarrow L_{X}X\hat{M}}_{\epsilon^{1/4}}(\rho^{L_{X}XM})

\displaystyle~~~~~~~~((T^{L_{X}XM\rightarrow L_{X}X\hat{M}}_{\epsilon^{1/4}}(\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{L_{X}XM})-T^{L_{X}XM\rightarrow L_{X}X\hat{M}}_{\epsilon^{1/4}}(\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{L_{X}}\otimes(\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{XM}-\Pi^{XM}(1))))\circ\hat{\hat{\rho}}^{L_{X}X\hat{M}})]

\displaystyle~~~{}+4\epsilon^{1/8}L^{-3/2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Tr[T^{L_{X}XM\rightarrow L_{X}X\hat{M}}_{\epsilon^{1/4}}((\frac{\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{L_{X}}}{L})\otimes\rho^{XM})

\displaystyle~~~~~~~~((T^{L_{X}XM\rightarrow L_{X}X\hat{M}}_{\epsilon^{1/4}}(\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{L_{X}}\otimes\Pi^{XM}(1)))\circ\hat{\hat{\rho}}^{L_{X}X\hat{M}})]+4\epsilon^{1/8}L^{-3/2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Tr[(T^{L_{X}XM\rightarrow L_{X}X\hat{M}}_{\epsilon^{1/4}}((\frac{\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{L_{X}}}{L})\otimes(\Pi^{XM}(1)\circ\rho^{XM}))\hat{\hat{\rho}}^{L_{X}X\hat{M}})]+4\epsilon^{1/8}L^{-3/2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|T^{L_{X}XM\rightarrow L_{X}X\hat{M}}_{\epsilon^{1/4}}((\frac{\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{L_{X}}}{L})\otimes(\Pi^{XM}(1)\circ\rho^{XM}))\|_{\infty}\cdot\|\hat{\hat{\rho}}^{L_{X}X\hat{M}}\|_{1}+4\epsilon^{1/8}L^{-3/2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\|(\frac{\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{L_{X}}}{L})\otimes(\Pi^{XM}(1)\circ\rho^{XM})\|_{\infty}\cdot\|\hat{\hat{\rho}}^{L_{X}X\hat{M}}\|_{1}+4\epsilon^{1/8}L^{-3/2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle L^{-1}\cdot\|\Pi^{XM}(1)\circ\rho^{XM}\|_{\infty}\cdot\|\hat{\hat{\rho}}^{L_{X}X\hat{M}}\|_{1}+4\epsilon^{1/8}L^{-3/2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{(1+3\sqrt{\epsilon})2^{D^{\epsilon}_{\infty}(\rho^{XM}\|\alpha^{X}\otimes\rho^{M})}}{(L|F^{\prime}_{\alpha}|)|F^{\prime}_{\rho}|}+4\epsilon^{1/8}L^{-3/2}.

Above, we used the fact that

\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{L_{X}XL_{Y}Y\hat{M}}-T^{L_{X}XL_{Y}YM\rightarrow L_{X}XL_{Y}Y\hat{M}}_{\epsilon^{1/4}}(\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{L_{X}L_{Y}}\otimes(\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{XYM}-\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{Y}\Pi^{XM}(1)))\geq\hat{\Pi}^{L_{X}XL_{Y}Y\hat{M}}

in the third equality,

\displaystyle(\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{L_{X}X\hat{M}}-T^{L_{X}XM\rightarrow L_{X}X\hat{M}}_{\epsilon^{1/4}}(\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{L_{X}}\otimes(\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{XM}-\Pi^{XM}(1))))(T^{L_{X}XM\rightarrow L_{X}X\hat{M}}_{\epsilon^{1/4}}(\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{L_{X}XM}))

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle T^{L_{X}XM\rightarrow L_{X}X\hat{M}}_{\epsilon^{1/4}}(\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{L_{X}XM})-T^{L_{X}XM\rightarrow L_{X}X\hat{M}}_{\epsilon^{1/4}}(\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{L_{X}}\otimes(\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{XM}-\Pi^{XM}(1)))

in the seventh equality,

T^{L_{X}XM\rightarrow L_{X}X\hat{M}}_{\epsilon^{1/4}}
is
an isometry in the ninth and tenth equalities.
The proof of the lemma is now complete.

\square

Lemma 11

\Tr[\hat{\rho}^{L_{Y}Y\hat{M}}\hat{\hat{\rho}}^{L_{Y}Y\hat{M}}]\leq\frac{4\epsilon^{1/8}}{L^{3/2}}+\frac{(1+3\sqrt{\epsilon})2^{D^{\epsilon}_{\infty}(\rho^{YM}\|\beta^{Y}\otimes\rho^{M})}}{(L|F^{\prime}_{\beta}|)|F^{\prime}_{\rho}|}.

Proof:

Similar to proof of Lemma
10
above.

\square

Lemma 12

\Tr[\hat{\rho}^{\hat{M}}\hat{\hat{\rho}}^{\hat{M}}]\leq\frac{8\epsilon^{1/8}}{L^{1/2}}+\frac{1+\sqrt{\epsilon}}{|F^{\prime}_{\rho}|}.

Proof:

Using Equation
16
and Lemma
4
, we get

\displaystyle\Tr[\hat{\rho}^{\hat{M}}\hat{\hat{\rho}}^{\hat{M}}]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Tr[\rho^{\hat{M}}\hat{\hat{\rho}}^{\hat{M}}]+\Tr[(\hat{\rho}^{\hat{M}}-\rho^{\hat{M}})\hat{\hat{\rho}}^{\hat{M}}]\;\leq\;\frac{1+\sqrt{\epsilon}}{|F^{\prime}_{\rho}|}\Tr[\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{F^{\prime}_{\rho}}\hat{\hat{\rho}}^{\hat{M}}]+\|\hat{\rho}^{\hat{M}}-\rho^{\hat{M}}\|_{\infty}\cdot\|\hat{\hat{\rho}}^{\hat{M}}\|_{1}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1+\sqrt{\epsilon}}{|F^{\prime}_{\rho}|}\Tr[\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{\hat{M}}\hat{\hat{\rho}}^{\hat{M}}]+8\epsilon^{1/8}L^{-1/2}\;\leq\;\frac{1+\sqrt{\epsilon}}{|F^{\prime}_{\rho}|}+8\epsilon^{1/8}L^{-1/2}.

This completes the proof of the lemma.

\square

By Lemma
2
and Equation
18
, we have

\|\sigma^{(L_{X}X)^{A}(L_{Y}Y)^{B}\hat{M}}-\hat{\sigma}^{(L_{X}X)^{A}(L_{Y}Y)^{B}\hat{M}}\|_{1}\leq\|\rho^{L_{X}XL_{Y}Y\hat{M}}-\hat{\rho}^{L_{X}XL_{Y}Y\hat{M}}\|_{1}=2\sqrt{2}\cdot\epsilon^{1/8}.

(21)

Hence by triangle inequality and Equation
19
, in
order to prove
Lemma
7
, it suffices to show that

\|\hat{\sigma}^{(L_{X}X)^{A}(L_{Y}Y)^{B}\hat{M}}-\tau^{(L_{X}X)^{A}(L_{Y}Y)^{B}\hat{M}}\|_{1}\leq 14\epsilon^{1/32}.

(22)

By Corollary
2
and Equation
18
,
we have

\displaystyle\|\hat{\sigma}^{(L_{X}X)^{A}(L_{Y}Y)^{B}\hat{M}}-\hat{\hat{\sigma}}^{(L_{X}X)^{A}(L_{Y}Y)^{B}\hat{M}}\|_{1}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|\hat{\rho}^{L_{X}XL_{Y}Y\hat{M}}-\hat{\hat{\rho}}^{L_{X}XL_{Y}Y\hat{M}}\|_{1}\;<\;25\cdot\epsilon^{1/8}.

By Lemma
1
, there is a subnormalised state

\hat{\sigma}^{{}^{\prime}(L_{X}X)^{A}(L_{Y}Y)^{B}\hat{M}}
such that

\begin{array}[]{c}\hat{\sigma}^{{}^{\prime}(L_{X}X)^{A}(L_{Y}Y)^{B}\hat{M}}\leq\hat{\sigma}^{(L_{X}X)^{A}(L_{Y}Y)^{B}\hat{M}},\\
\|\hat{\sigma}^{{}^{\prime}(L_{X}X)^{A}(L_{Y}Y)^{B}\hat{M}}-\hat{\sigma}^{(L_{X}X)^{A}(L_{Y}Y)^{B}\hat{M}}\|_{1}<5\epsilon^{1/16},\\
\|\hat{\sigma}^{{}^{\prime}(L_{X}X)^{A}(L_{Y}Y)^{B}\hat{M}}\|_{2}^{2}\leq(1+10\epsilon^{1/16})\Tr[\hat{\sigma}^{(L_{X}X)^{A}(L_{Y}Y)^{B}\hat{M}}\hat{\hat{\sigma}}^{(L_{X}X)^{A}(L_{Y}Y)^{B}\hat{M}}].\end{array}

(23)

Hence by triangle inequality and Equation
22
, in
order to prove
Lemma
7
, it suffices to show that

\|\hat{\sigma}^{{}^{\prime}(L_{X}X)^{A}(L_{Y}Y)^{B}\hat{M}}-\tau^{(L_{X}X)^{A}(L_{Y}Y)^{B}\hat{M}}\|_{1}\leq 9\epsilon^{1/32}.

(24)

Note that

\hat{\sigma}^{{}^{\prime}(L_{X}X)^{A}(L_{Y}Y)^{B}\hat{M}}
is subnormalised.

Define
\Pi^{(L_{X}X)^{A}(L_{Y}Y)^{B}\hat{M}}
to be the orthogonal projector
from the ambient space
(L_{X}X)^{A}(L_{Y}Y)^{B}\hat{M}
onto

\mathrm{supp}(\tau^{(L_{X}X)^{A}(L_{Y}Y)^{B}\hat{M}})=(L_{X}\otimes F^{\prime}_{\alpha})^{A}\otimes(L_{Y}\otimes F^{\prime}_{\beta})^{B}\otimes F^{\prime}_{\rho}\geq\mathrm{supp}(\sigma^{(L_{X}X)^{A}(L_{Y}Y)^{B}\hat{M}}).

By Equations
23
,
21
, we get

\begin{array}[]{rcl}\lx@intercol\lx@add@cssclass{ltx_eqn_lefteqn}$\displaystyle\Tr[\Pi^{(L_{X}X)^{A}(L_{Y}Y)^{B}\hat{M}}\tau^{(L_{X}X)^{A}(L_{Y}Y)^{B}\hat{M}}]$\mbox{}\hfil\lx@intercol\\
&=&\Tr[\tau^{(L_{X}X)^{A}(L_{Y}Y)^{B}\hat{M}}]\;=\;1,\\
\lx@intercol\lx@add@cssclass{ltx_eqn_lefteqn}$\displaystyle\Tr[\Pi^{(L_{X}X)^{A}(L_{Y}Y)^{B}\hat{M}}\hat{\sigma}^{{}^{\prime}(L_{X}X)^{A}(L_{Y}Y)^{B}\hat{M}}]$\mbox{}\hfil\lx@intercol\\
&\geq&\Tr[\Pi^{(L_{X}X)^{A}(L_{Y}Y)^{B}\hat{M}}\sigma^{(L_{X}X)^{A}(L_{Y}Y)^{B}\hat{M}}]-\|\sigma^{(L_{X}X)^{A}(L_{Y}Y)^{B}\hat{M}}-\hat{\sigma}^{(L_{X}X)^{A}(L_{Y}Y)^{B}\hat{M}}\|_{1}\\
&&~~~~~~{}-\|\hat{\sigma}^{(L_{X}X)^{A}(L_{Y}Y)^{B}\hat{M}}-\hat{\sigma}^{{}^{\prime}(L_{X}X)^{A}(L_{Y}Y)^{B}\hat{M}}\|_{1}\\
&=&\Tr[\sigma^{(L_{X}X)^{A}(L_{Y}Y)^{B}\hat{M}}]-\|\sigma^{(L_{X}X)^{A}(L_{Y}Y)^{B}\hat{M}}-\hat{\sigma}^{(L_{X}X)^{A}(L_{Y}Y)^{B}\hat{M}}\|_{1}\\
&&~~~~~~{}-\|\hat{\sigma}^{(L_{X}X)^{A}(L_{Y}Y)^{B}\hat{M}}-\hat{\sigma}^{{}^{\prime}(L_{X}X)^{A}(L_{Y}Y)^{B}\hat{M}}\|_{1}\\
&\geq&1-2\sqrt{2}\cdot\epsilon^{1/8}-5\epsilon^{1/16}\;\geq\;1-8\epsilon^{1/16}.\end{array}

(25)

By Proposition
1
and
Equations
24
,
25
, in order to prove
Lemma
7
, it suffices to show that

\sqrt{\Tr[\Pi^{(L_{X}X)^{A}(L_{Y}Y)^{B}\hat{M}}]}\cdot\|\hat{\sigma}^{{}^{\prime}(L_{X}X)^{A}(L_{Y}Y)^{B}\hat{M}}-\tau^{(L_{X}X)^{A}(L_{Y}Y)^{B}\hat{M}}\|_{2}<6\epsilon^{1/32}.

Observe that

\Tr[\Pi^{(L_{X}X)^{A}(L_{Y}Y)^{B}\hat{M}}]=L^{A+B}|F^{\prime}_{\alpha}|^{A}|F^{\prime}_{\beta}|^{B}|F^{\prime}_{\rho}|.

Hence it suffices to show that

\|\hat{\sigma}^{{}^{\prime}(L_{X}X)^{A}(L_{Y}Y)^{B}\hat{M}}-\tau^{(L_{X}X)^{A}(L_{Y}Y)^{B}\hat{M}}\|_{2}^{2}<\frac{35\epsilon^{1/16}}{L^{A+B}|F^{\prime}_{\alpha}|^{A}|F^{\prime}_{\beta}|^{B}|F^{\prime}_{\rho}|}.

(26)

The left hand side of the above inequality is

\begin{array}[]{rcl}\lx@intercol\lx@add@cssclass{ltx_eqn_lefteqn}$\displaystyle\|\hat{\sigma}^{{}^{\prime}(L_{X}X)^{A}(L_{Y}Y)^{B}\hat{M}}-\tau^{(L_{X}X)^{A}(L_{Y}Y)^{B}\hat{M}}\|_{2}^{2}$\mbox{}\hfil\lx@intercol\\
&=&\|\hat{\sigma}^{{}^{\prime}(L_{X}X)^{A}(L_{Y}Y)^{B}\hat{M}}\|_{2}^{2}+\|\tau^{(L_{X}X)^{A}(L_{Y}Y)^{B}\hat{M}}\|_{2}^{2}\\
&.&~~~{}-2\Tr[\hat{\sigma}^{{}^{\prime}(L_{X}X)^{A}(L_{Y}Y)^{B}\hat{M}}\tau^{(L_{X}X)^{A}(L_{Y}Y)^{B}\hat{M}}]\\
&\leq&(1+10\epsilon^{1/16})\Tr[\hat{\sigma}^{(L_{X}X)^{A}(L_{Y}Y)^{B}\hat{M}}\hat{\hat{\sigma}}^{(L_{X}X)^{A}(L_{Y}Y)^{B}\hat{M}}]\\
&&~~~~{}+(1+\sqrt{\epsilon})^{2}\left\|\frac{(\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{L_{X}F^{\prime}_{\alpha}})^{\otimes A}\otimes(\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{L_{Y}F^{\prime}_{\beta}})^{\otimes B}\otimes(\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{F^{\prime}_{\rho}})}{(L|F^{\prime}_{\alpha}|)^{A}(L|F^{\prime}_{\beta}|)^{B}|F^{\prime}_{\rho}|}\right\|_{2}^{2}\\
&&~~~~{}-2(1-\sqrt{\epsilon})\Tr\left[\hat{\sigma}^{{}^{\prime}(L_{X}X)^{A}(L_{Y}Y)^{B}\hat{M}}\left(\frac{(\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{L_{X}F^{\prime}_{\alpha}})^{\otimes A}\otimes(\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{L_{Y}F^{\prime}_{\beta}})^{\otimes B}\otimes(\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{F^{\prime}_{\rho}})}{(L|F^{\prime}_{\alpha}|)^{A}(L|F^{\prime}_{\beta}|)^{B}|F^{\prime}_{\rho}|}\right)\right]\\
&=&(1+10\epsilon^{1/16})\Tr[\hat{\sigma}^{(L_{X}X)^{A}(L_{Y}Y)^{B}\hat{M}}\hat{\hat{\sigma}}^{(L_{X}X)^{A}(L_{Y}Y)^{B}\hat{M}}]+\frac{(1+\sqrt{\epsilon})^{2}}{(L|F^{\prime}_{\alpha}|)^{A}(L|F^{\prime}_{\beta}|)^{B}|F^{\prime}_{\rho}|}\\
&&~~~~{}-\frac{2(1-\sqrt{\epsilon})}{(L|F^{\prime}_{\alpha}|)^{A}(L|F^{\prime}_{\beta}|)^{B}|F^{\prime}_{\rho}|}\Tr[\hat{\sigma}^{{}^{\prime}(L_{X}X)^{A}(L_{Y}Y)^{B}\hat{M}}\Pi^{(L_{X}X)^{A}(L_{Y}Y)^{B}\hat{M}}]\\
&\leq&(1+10\epsilon^{1/16})\Tr[\hat{\sigma}^{(L_{X}X)^{A}(L_{Y}Y)^{B}\hat{M}}\hat{\hat{\sigma}}^{(L_{X}X)^{A}(L_{Y}Y)^{B}\hat{M}}]+\frac{(1+\sqrt{\epsilon})^{2}}{(L|F^{\prime}_{\alpha}|)^{A}(L|F^{\prime}_{\beta}|)^{B}|F^{\prime}_{\rho}|}\\
&&~~~~{}-\frac{2(1-\sqrt{\epsilon})(1-8\epsilon^{1/16})}{(L|F^{\prime}_{\alpha}|)^{A}(L|F^{\prime}_{\beta}|)^{B}|F^{\prime}_{\rho}|}\\
&\leq&(1+10\epsilon^{1/16})\Tr[\hat{\sigma}^{(L_{X}X)^{A}(L_{Y}Y)^{B}\hat{M}}\hat{\hat{\sigma}}^{(L_{X}X)^{A}(L_{Y}Y)^{B}\hat{M}}]+\frac{12\epsilon^{1/16}-1}{(L|F^{\prime}_{\alpha}|)^{A}(L|F^{\prime}_{\beta}|)^{B}|F^{\prime}_{\rho}|},\end{array}

(27)

where the first inequality follows from
Equations
23
,
20
, and the
second inequality follows from Equation
25
.

From Equations
26
and
27
,
in order to prove
Lemma
7
, it suffices to show the
following lemma.

Lemma 13

Suppose

\sqrt{L}>\max\left\{\frac{4|F^{\prime}_{\beta}||F^{\prime}_{\rho}|}{\epsilon^{3/8}2^{D^{\epsilon}_{\infty}(\rho^{YM}\|\beta^{Y}\otimes\rho^{M})}},\frac{4|F^{\prime}_{\alpha}||F^{\prime}_{\rho}|}{\epsilon^{3/8}2^{D^{\epsilon}_{\infty}(\rho^{XM}\|\alpha^{X}\otimes\rho^{M})}},\frac{8|F^{\prime}_{\rho}|}{\epsilon^{3/8}}\right\},

and

\displaystyle\log A

\displaystyle>

\displaystyle D^{\epsilon}_{\infty}(\rho^{XM}\|\alpha^{X}\otimes\rho^{M})+\log\epsilon^{-1/16},

\displaystyle\log B

\displaystyle>

\displaystyle D^{\epsilon}_{\infty}(\rho^{YM}\|\beta^{Y}\otimes\rho^{M})+\log\epsilon^{-1/16},

\displaystyle\log A+\log B

\displaystyle>

\displaystyle D^{\epsilon}_{\infty}(\rho^{XYM}\|\alpha^{X}\otimes\beta^{Y}\otimes\rho^{M})+\log\epsilon^{-1/16}.

Then,

\Tr[\hat{\sigma}^{(L_{X}X)^{A}(L_{Y}Y)^{B}\hat{M}}\hat{\hat{\sigma}}^{(L_{X}X)^{A}(L_{Y}Y)^{B}\hat{M}}]<\frac{1+11\epsilon^{1/16}}{(L|F^{\prime}_{\alpha}|)^{A}(L|F^{\prime}_{\beta}|)^{B}|F^{\prime}_{\rho}|}.

Proof:

Given the lower bound on
\sqrt{L}
, we can write down the following
consequences of Lemmas
12
,

10
,
11
,
9
.

\begin{array}[]{rcl}\Tr[\hat{\rho}^{\hat{M}}\hat{\hat{\rho}}^{\hat{M}}]&\leq&\frac{1+2\sqrt{\epsilon}}{|F^{\prime}_{\rho}|},\\
\Tr[\hat{\rho}^{L_{X}X\hat{M}}\hat{\hat{\rho}}^{L_{X}X\hat{M}}]&\leq&\frac{(1+4\sqrt{\epsilon})2^{D^{\epsilon}_{\infty}(\rho^{XM}\|\alpha^{X}\otimes\rho^{M})}}{(L|F^{\prime}_{\alpha}|)|F^{\prime}_{\rho}|},\\
\Tr[\hat{\rho}^{L_{Y}Y\hat{M}}\hat{\hat{\rho}}^{L_{Y}Y\hat{M}}]&\leq&\frac{(1+4\sqrt{\epsilon})2^{D^{\epsilon}_{\infty}(\rho^{YM}\|\beta^{Y}\otimes\rho^{M})}}{(LF^{\prime}_{\beta})|F^{\prime}_{\rho}|},\\
\Tr[\hat{\rho}^{L_{X}XL_{Y}Y\hat{M}}\hat{\hat{\rho}}^{L_{X}XL_{Y}Y\hat{M}}]&\leq&\frac{(1+3\sqrt{\epsilon})2^{D^{\epsilon}_{\infty}(\rho^{XYM}\|\alpha^{X}\otimes\beta^{Y}\otimes\rho^{M})}}{(L|F^{\prime}_{\alpha}|)(LF^{\prime}_{\beta})|F^{\prime}_{\rho}|},\\
\end{array}

(28)

We have,

\begin{array}[]{rcl}\lx@intercol\lx@add@cssclass{ltx_eqn_lefteqn}$\displaystyle\Tr[\hat{\sigma}^{(L_{X}X)^{A}(L_{Y}Y)^{B}\hat{M}}\hat{\hat{\sigma}}^{(L_{X}X)^{A}(L_{Y}Y)^{B}\hat{M}}]$\mbox{}\hfil\lx@intercol\\
&=&(AB)^{-2}\cdot\Tr\left[\sum_{a,\hat{a}=1}^{A}\sum_{b,\hat{b}=1}^{B}(\hat{\rho}^{(L_{X}X)_{a}(L_{Y}Y)_{b}\hat{M}}\otimes\alpha^{(L_{X}X)^{-a}}\otimes\beta^{(L_{Y}Y)^{-b}})\right.\\
&&~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~\left.(\hat{\hat{\rho}}^{(L_{X}X)_{\hat{a}}(L_{Y}Y)_{\hat{b}}\hat{M}}\otimes\alpha^{(L_{X}X)^{-\hat{a}}}\otimes\beta^{(L_{Y}Y)^{-\hat{b}}})\right]\\
&\leq&\frac{(1+\sqrt{\epsilon})^{2}}{(AB)^{2}(L|F^{\prime}_{\alpha}|)^{2(A-1)}(L|F^{\prime}_{\beta}|)^{2(B-1)}}\\
&&~~~~{}\cdot\sum_{a,\hat{a}=1}^{A}\sum_{b,\hat{b}=1}^{B}\Tr[(\hat{\rho}^{(L_{X}X)_{a}(L_{Y}Y)_{b}\hat{M}}\otimes\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{(L_{X}F^{\prime}_{\alpha})^{-a}}\otimes\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{(L_{Y}F^{\prime}_{\beta})^{-b}})\\
&&~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~(\hat{\hat{\rho}}^{(L_{X}X)_{\hat{a}}(L_{Y}Y)_{\hat{b}}\hat{M}}\otimes\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{(L_{X}F^{\prime}_{\alpha})^{-\hat{a}}}\otimes\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{(L_{Y}F^{\prime}_{\beta})^{-\hat{b}}})]\\
\end{array}

(29)

We analyse the above summation by considering several cases.
Consider the following term for a fixed choice of

a\neq\hat{a}
,
b\neq\hat{b}
.

\begin{array}[]{rcl}\lx@intercol\lx@add@cssclass{ltx_eqn_lefteqn}$\displaystyle\Tr[(\hat{\rho}^{(L_{X}X)_{a}(L_{Y}Y)_{b}\hat{M}}\otimes\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{(L_{X}F^{\prime}_{\alpha})^{-a}}\otimes\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{(L_{Y}F^{\prime}_{\beta})^{-b}})(\hat{\hat{\rho}}^{(L_{X}X)_{\hat{a}}(L_{Y}Y)_{\hat{b}}\hat{M}}\otimes\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{(L_{X}F^{\prime}_{\alpha})^{-\hat{a}}}\otimes\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{(L_{Y}F^{\prime}_{\beta})^{-\hat{b}}})]$\mbox{}\hfil\lx@intercol\\
&=&\Tr[(\hat{\rho}^{(L_{X}X)_{a}(L_{Y}Y)_{b}\hat{M}}\otimes\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{(L_{X}F^{\prime}_{\alpha})_{\hat{a}}}\otimes\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{(L_{Y}F^{\prime}_{\beta})_{\hat{b}}}\otimes\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{(L_{X}F^{\prime}_{\alpha})^{-a,\hat{a}}}\otimes\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{(L_{Y}F^{\prime}_{\beta})^{-b,\hat{b}}})\\
&&~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~(\hat{\hat{\rho}}^{(L_{X}X)_{\hat{a}}(L_{Y}Y)_{\hat{b}}\hat{M}}\otimes\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{(L_{X}F^{\prime}_{\alpha})_{a}}\otimes\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{(L_{Y}F^{\prime}_{\beta})_{b}}\otimes\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{(L_{X}F^{\prime}_{\alpha})^{-a,\hat{a}}}\otimes\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{(L_{Y}F^{\prime}_{\beta})^{-b,\hat{b}}})]\\
&=&\Tr[((\hat{\rho}^{(L_{X}X)_{a}(L_{Y}Y)_{b}\hat{M}}\otimes\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{(L_{X}F^{\prime}_{\alpha})_{\hat{a}}}\otimes\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{(L_{Y}F^{\prime}_{\beta})_{\hat{b}}})\\
&&~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~(\hat{\hat{\rho}}^{(L_{X}X)_{\hat{a}}(L_{Y}Y)_{\hat{b}}\hat{M}}\otimes\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{(L_{X}F^{\prime}_{\alpha})_{a}}\otimes\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{(L_{Y}F^{\prime}_{\beta})_{b}}))\otimes\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{(L_{X}F^{\prime}_{\alpha})^{-a,\hat{a}}}\otimes\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{(L_{Y}F^{\prime}_{\beta})^{-b,\hat{b}}}]\\
&=&(L|F^{\prime}_{\alpha}|)^{A-2}(L|F^{\prime}_{\beta}|)^{B-2}\\
&&~~~~{}\cdot\Tr[(\hat{\rho}^{(L_{X}X)_{a}(L_{Y}Y)_{b}\hat{M}}\otimes\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{(L_{X}F^{\prime}_{\alpha})_{\hat{a}}}\otimes\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{(L_{Y}F^{\prime}_{\beta})_{\hat{b}}})\\
&&~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~(\hat{\hat{\rho}}^{(L_{X}X)_{\hat{a}}(L_{Y}Y)_{\hat{b}}\hat{M}}\otimes\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{(L_{X}F^{\prime}_{\alpha})_{a}}\otimes\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{(L_{Y}F^{\prime}_{\beta})_{b}})]\\
&=&(L|F^{\prime}_{\alpha}|)^{A-2}(L|F^{\prime}_{\beta}|)^{B-2}\\
&&~~~~{}\cdot\Tr[(\hat{\rho}^{(L_{X}X)_{a}(L_{Y}Y)_{b}\hat{M}}\otimes\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{(L_{X}X)_{\hat{a}}}\otimes\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{(L_{Y}Y)_{\hat{b}}})\\
&&~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~(\hat{\hat{\rho}}^{(L_{X}X)_{\hat{a}}(L_{Y}Y)_{\hat{b}}\hat{M}}\otimes\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{(L_{X}X)_{a}}\otimes\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{(L_{Y}Y)_{b}})]\\
&=&(L|F^{\prime}_{\alpha}|)^{A-2}(L|F^{\prime}_{\beta}|)^{B-2}\Tr[\hat{\rho}^{\hat{M}}\hat{\hat{\rho}}^{\hat{M}}]\;\leq\;\frac{(1+2\sqrt{\epsilon})(L|F^{\prime}_{\alpha}|)^{A-2}(L|F^{\prime}_{\beta}|)^{B-2}}{|F^{\prime}_{\rho}|},\end{array}

(30)

where we used Equation
28
in the inequality above.
There are
A(A-1)B(B-1)
such terms.

Consider the following term for a fixed choice of

a=\hat{a}
,
b\neq\hat{b}
.

\begin{array}[]{rcl}\lx@intercol\lx@add@cssclass{ltx_eqn_lefteqn}$\displaystyle\Tr[(\hat{\rho}^{(L_{X}X)_{a}(L_{Y}Y)_{b}\hat{M}}\otimes\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{(L_{X}F^{\prime}_{\alpha})^{-a}}\otimes\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{(L_{Y}F^{\prime}_{\beta})^{-b}})(\hat{\hat{\rho}}^{(L_{X}X)_{a}(L_{Y}Y)_{\hat{b}}\hat{M}}\otimes\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{(L_{X}F^{\prime}_{\alpha})^{-a}}\otimes\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{(L_{Y}F^{\prime}_{\beta})^{-\hat{b}}})]$\mbox{}\hfil\lx@intercol\\
&=&\Tr[(\hat{\rho}^{(L_{X}X)_{a}(L_{Y}Y)_{b}\hat{M}}\otimes\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{(L_{Y}F^{\prime}_{\beta})_{\hat{b}}}\otimes\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{(L_{X}F^{\prime}_{\alpha})^{-a}}\otimes\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{(L_{Y}F^{\prime}_{\beta})^{-b,\hat{b}}})\\
&&~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~(\hat{\hat{\rho}}^{(L_{X}X)_{a}(L_{Y}Y)_{\hat{b}}\hat{M}}\otimes\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{(L_{Y}F^{\prime}_{\beta})_{b}}\otimes\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{(L_{X}F^{\prime}_{\alpha})^{-a}}\otimes\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{(L_{Y}F^{\prime}_{\beta})^{-b,\hat{b}}})]\\
&=&\Tr[((\hat{\rho}^{(L_{X}X)_{a}(L_{Y}Y)_{b}\hat{M}}\otimes\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{(L_{Y}F^{\prime}_{\beta})_{\hat{b}}})\\
&&~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~(\hat{\hat{\rho}}^{(L_{X}X)_{a}(L_{Y}Y)_{\hat{b}}\hat{M}}\otimes\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{(L_{Y}F^{\prime}_{\beta})_{b}}))\otimes\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{(L_{X}F^{\prime}_{\alpha})^{-a}}\otimes\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{(L_{Y}F^{\prime}_{\beta})^{-b,\hat{b}}}]\\
&=&(L|F^{\prime}_{\alpha}|)^{A-1}(L|F^{\prime}_{\beta}|)^{B-2}\Tr[(\hat{\rho}^{(L_{X}X)_{a}(L_{Y}Y)_{b}\hat{M}}\otimes\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{(L_{Y}F^{\prime}_{\beta})_{\hat{b}}})\\
&&~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~(\hat{\hat{\rho}}^{(L_{X}X)_{a}(L_{Y}Y)_{\hat{b}}\hat{M}}\otimes\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{(L_{Y}F^{\prime}_{\beta})_{b}})]\\
&=&(L|F^{\prime}_{\alpha}|)^{A-1}(L|F^{\prime}_{\beta}|)^{B-2}\Tr[(\hat{\rho}^{(L_{X}X)_{a}(L_{Y}Y)_{b}\hat{M}}\otimes\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{(L_{Y}Y)_{\hat{b}}})(\hat{\hat{\rho}}^{(L_{X}X)_{\hat{a}}(L_{Y}Y)_{\hat{b}}\hat{M}}\otimes\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{(L_{Y}Y)_{b}})]\\
&=&(L|F^{\prime}_{\alpha}|)^{A-1}(L|F^{\prime}_{\beta}|)^{B-2}\Tr[\hat{\rho}^{(L_{X}X)\hat{M}}\hat{\hat{\rho}}^{(L_{X}X)\hat{M}}]\\
&\leq&\frac{(1+4\sqrt{\epsilon})(L|F^{\prime}_{\alpha}|)^{A-1}(L|F^{\prime}_{\beta}|)^{B-2}2^{D^{\epsilon}_{\infty}(\rho^{XM}\|\alpha^{X}\otimes\rho^{M})}}{L|F^{\prime}_{\alpha}||F^{\prime}_{\rho}|},\end{array}

(31)

where we used Equation
28
in the inequality above.
There are
AB(B-1)
such terms.

Consider the following term for a fixed choice of

a\neq\hat{a}
,
b=\hat{b}
. We have similarly,

\begin{array}[]{rcl}\lx@intercol\lx@add@cssclass{ltx_eqn_lefteqn}$\displaystyle\Tr[(\hat{\rho}^{(L_{X}X)_{a}(L_{Y}Y)_{b}\hat{M}}\otimes\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{(L_{X}F^{\prime}_{\alpha})^{-a}}\otimes\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{(L_{Y}F^{\prime}_{\beta})^{-b}})$\mbox{}\hfil\lx@intercol\\
&&~~~~~~~~(\hat{\hat{\rho}}^{(L_{X}X)_{\hat{a}}(L_{Y}Y)_{b}\hat{M}}\otimes\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{(L_{X}F^{\prime}_{\alpha})^{-\hat{a}}}\otimes\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{(L_{Y}F^{\prime}_{\beta})^{-b}})]\\
&\leq&\frac{(1+4\sqrt{\epsilon})(L|F^{\prime}_{\alpha}|)^{A-2}(L|F^{\prime}_{\beta}|)^{B-1}2^{D^{\epsilon}_{\infty}(\rho^{YM}\|\beta^{Y}\otimes\rho^{M})}}{L|F^{\prime}_{\beta}||F^{\prime}_{\rho}|}.\end{array}

(32)

There are
A(A-1)B
such terms.

Finally, consider the following term for a fixed choice of

a=\hat{a}
,
b=\hat{b}
.

\begin{array}[]{rcl}\lx@intercol\lx@add@cssclass{ltx_eqn_lefteqn}$\displaystyle\Tr[(\hat{\rho}^{(L_{X}X)_{a}(L_{Y}Y)_{b}\hat{M}}\otimes\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{(L_{X}F^{\prime}_{\alpha})^{-a}}\otimes\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{(L_{Y}F^{\prime}_{\beta})^{-b}})$\mbox{}\hfil\lx@intercol\\
&&~~~~~~(\hat{\hat{\rho}}^{(L_{X}X)_{a}(L_{Y}Y)_{b}\hat{M}}\otimes\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{(L_{X}F^{\prime}_{\alpha})^{-a}}\otimes\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{(L_{Y}F^{\prime}_{\beta})^{-b}})]\\
&=&\Tr[(\hat{\rho}^{(L_{X}X)_{a}(L_{Y}Y)_{b}\hat{M}}\hat{\hat{\rho}}^{(L_{X}X)_{a}(L_{Y}Y)_{b}\hat{M}})\otimes\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{(L_{X}F^{\prime}_{\alpha})^{-a}}\otimes\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{(L_{Y}F^{\prime}_{\beta})^{-b}}]\\
&=&(L|F^{\prime}_{\alpha}|)^{A-1}(L|F^{\prime}_{\beta}|)^{B-1}\Tr[\hat{\rho}^{(L_{X}X)_{a}(L_{Y}Y)_{b}\hat{M}}\hat{\hat{\rho}}^{(L_{X}X)_{a}(L_{Y}Y)_{b}\hat{M}}]\\
&\leq&\frac{(1+3\sqrt{\epsilon})(L|F^{\prime}_{\alpha}|)^{A-1}(L|F^{\prime}_{\beta}|)^{B-1}2^{D^{\epsilon}_{\infty}(\rho^{XYM}\|\alpha^{X}\otimes\beta^{Y}\otimes\rho^{M})}}{(L|F^{\prime}_{\alpha}|)(L|F^{\prime}_{\beta}|)|F^{\prime}_{\rho}|},\end{array}

(33)

where we used Equation
28
in the inequality above.
There are
AB
such terms.

From Equations
30
,
31
,

32
,
33
we get

\begin{array}[]{rcl}\lx@intercol\lx@add@cssclass{ltx_eqn_lefteqn}$\displaystyle\sum_{a,\hat{a}=1}^{A}\sum_{b,\hat{b}=1}^{B}\Tr[(\hat{\rho}^{(L_{X}X)_{a}(L_{Y}Y)_{b}\hat{M}}\otimes\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{(L_{X}F^{\prime}_{\alpha})^{-a}}\otimes\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{(L_{Y}F^{\prime}_{\beta})^{-b}})$\mbox{}\hfil\lx@intercol\\
&&~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~(\hat{\hat{\rho}}^{(L_{X}X)_{\hat{a}}(L_{Y}Y)_{\hat{b}}\hat{M}}\otimes\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{(L_{X}F^{\prime}_{\alpha})^{-\hat{a}}}\otimes\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{(L_{Y}F^{\prime}_{\beta})^{-\hat{b}}})]\\
&=&\frac{A(A-1)B(B-1)(1+2\sqrt{\epsilon})(L|F^{\prime}_{\alpha}|)^{A-2}(L|F^{\prime}_{\beta}|)^{B-2}}{|F^{\prime}_{\rho}|}\\
&&~~~~{}+\frac{AB(B-1)(1+4\sqrt{\epsilon})(L|F^{\prime}_{\alpha}|)^{A-1}(L|F^{\prime}_{\beta}|)^{B-2}2^{D^{\epsilon}_{\infty}(\rho^{XM}\|\alpha^{X}\otimes\rho^{M})}}{L|F^{\prime}_{\alpha}||F^{\prime}_{\rho}|}\\
&&~~~~{}+\frac{A(A-1)B(1+4\sqrt{\epsilon})(L|F^{\prime}_{\alpha}|)^{A-2}(L|F^{\prime}_{\beta}|)^{B-1}2^{D^{\epsilon}_{\infty}(\rho^{YM}\|\beta^{Y}\otimes\rho^{M})}}{L|F^{\prime}_{\beta}||F^{\prime}_{\rho}|}\\
&&~~~~{}+\frac{AB(1+3\sqrt{\epsilon})(L|F^{\prime}_{\alpha}|)^{A-1}(L|F^{\prime}_{\beta}|)^{B-1}2^{D^{\epsilon}_{\infty}(\rho^{XYM}\|\alpha^{X}\otimes\beta^{Y}\otimes\rho^{M})}}{(L|F^{\prime}_{\alpha}|)(L|F^{\prime}_{\beta}|)|F^{\prime}_{\rho}|}.\end{array}

(34)

From Equations
29
,
34
we get,

\displaystyle\Tr[\hat{\sigma}^{(L_{X}X)^{A}(L_{Y}Y)^{B}\hat{M}}\hat{\hat{\sigma}}^{(L_{X}X)^{A}(L_{Y}Y)^{B}\hat{M}}]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{(1+\sqrt{\epsilon})^{2}}{(AB)^{2}(L|F^{\prime}_{\alpha}|)^{2(A-1)}(L|F^{\prime}_{\beta}|)^{2(B-1)}}

\displaystyle~~~~{}\cdot\left[\frac{A(A-1)B(B-1)(1+2\sqrt{\epsilon})(L|F^{\prime}_{\alpha}|)^{A-2}(L|F^{\prime}_{\beta}|)^{B-2}}{|F^{\prime}_{\rho}|}\right.

\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~~~~{}+\frac{AB(B-1)(1+4\sqrt{\epsilon})(L|F^{\prime}_{\alpha}|)^{A-1}(L|F^{\prime}_{\beta}|)^{B-2}2^{D^{\epsilon}_{\infty}(\rho^{XM}\|\alpha^{X}\otimes\rho^{M})}}{L|F^{\prime}_{\alpha}||F^{\prime}_{\rho}|}

\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~~~~{}+\frac{A(A-1)B(1+4\sqrt{\epsilon})(L|F^{\prime}_{\alpha}|)^{A-2}(L|F^{\prime}_{\beta}|)^{B-1}2^{D^{\epsilon}_{\infty}(\rho^{YM}\|\beta^{Y}\otimes\rho^{M})}}{L|F^{\prime}_{\beta}||F^{\prime}_{\rho}|}

\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~~~~\left.{}+\frac{AB(1+3\sqrt{\epsilon})(L|F^{\prime}_{\alpha}|)^{A-1}(L|F^{\prime}_{\beta}|)^{B-1}2^{D^{\epsilon}_{\infty}(\rho^{XYM}\|\alpha^{X}\otimes\beta^{Y}\otimes\rho^{M})}}{(L|F^{\prime}_{\alpha}|)(L|F^{\prime}_{\beta}|)|F^{\prime}_{\rho}|}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle(1+7\sqrt{\epsilon})\left[\frac{1}{(L|F^{\prime}_{\alpha}|)^{A}(L|F^{\prime}_{\beta}|)^{B}|F^{\prime}_{\rho}|}+A^{-1}\cdot\frac{2^{D^{\epsilon}_{\infty}(\rho^{XM}\|\alpha^{X}\otimes\rho^{M})}}{(L|F^{\prime}_{\alpha}|)^{A}(L|F^{\prime}_{\beta}|)^{B}|F^{\prime}_{\rho}|}\right.

\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~\left.{}+B^{-1}\cdot\frac{2^{D^{\epsilon}_{\infty}(\rho^{YM}\|\beta^{Y}\otimes\rho^{M})}}{(L|F^{\prime}_{\alpha}|)^{A}(L|F^{\prime}_{\beta}|)^{B}|F^{\prime}_{\rho}|}+(AB)^{-1}\cdot\frac{2^{D^{\epsilon}_{\infty}(\rho^{XYM}\|\alpha^{X}\otimes\beta^{Y}\otimes\rho^{M})}}{(L|F^{\prime}_{\alpha}|)^{A}(L|F^{\prime}_{\beta}|)^{B}|F^{\prime}_{\rho}|}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{(1+7\sqrt{\epsilon})(1+3\epsilon^{1/16})}{(L|F^{\prime}_{\alpha}|)^{A}(L|F^{\prime}_{\beta}|)^{B}|F^{\prime}_{\rho}|}\;\leq\;\frac{1+11\epsilon^{1/16}}{(L|F^{\prime}_{\alpha}|)^{A}(L|F^{\prime}_{\beta}|)^{B}|F^{\prime}_{\rho}|}.

This finishes the proof of the lemma.

\square

Since the dimension
L
of the augmenting Hilbert spaces
L_{X}
,
L_{Y}

is a free parameter in our proof, it can be chosen large enough as
in the requirement of Lemma
13
. Thus
Lemma
13
holds, which completes the proof of
Lemma
7
.

5
Smooth soft covering without pairwise independence

We will now prove a smooth multipartite soft covering classical quantum
version of
Lemma
7
where pairwise independence amongst
the classical random variables can be relaxed slightly. For simplicity,
we will prove the case with two parties only. However, analogous results
hold for greater than two parties also, as the machinery will make amply
clear below.

Let
\mathcal{X}
,
\mathcal{Y}
be classical alphabets.
Let
p^{XY}
be a normalised probability distribution on
XY
, whose
marginals are denoted by
p^{X}
,
p^{Y}
.
Let
q^{X}
,
q^{Y}
be normalised probability distributions.
For each
(x,y)\in\mathcal{X}\times\mathcal{Y}
,
let
\rho^{M}_{xy}
be a subnormalised density matrix on
M
.
We define the following classical quantum
control state
:

\rho^{XYM}:=\sum_{(x,y)\in\mathcal{X}\times\mathcal{Y}}p^{XY}(x,y)|x,y\rangle\langle x,y|^{XY}\otimes\rho^{M}_{xy}.

Define the density matrices

\rho^{M}_{x}:=\sum_{y}p^{Y|X=x}(y)\rho^{M}_{xy},~\rho^{M}_{y}:=\sum_{x}p^{X|Y=y}(x)\rho^{M}_{xy},

and the classical quantum states

\rho^{XM,Y|X}:=\sum_{x,y}p^{XY}(x,y)|x,y\rangle\langle x,y|^{XY}\otimes\rho^{M}_{x},~\rho^{YM,X|Y}:=\sum_{x,y}p^{XY}(x,y)|x,y\rangle\langle x,y|^{XY}\otimes\rho^{M}_{y}.

Intuitively,
\rho^{M}_{x}
is the quantum state induced on
M
when the

X
register of the control state
\rho^{XYM}
is measured and found to
be equal to
x
. Similarly, the classical quantum state
\rho^{XM,Y|X}

is what one gets if one ‘decouples’ the register
M
from
Y
, making
it depend only on the contents of
X
, without
affecting the probability distribution on
XY
. Analogous remarks can
be made for
\rho^{M}_{y}
and
\rho^{YM,X|Y}
.

Suppose
\mathrm{supp}(p^{X})\leq\mathrm{supp}(q^{X})
and

\mathrm{supp}(p^{Y})\leq\mathrm{supp}(q^{Y})
. Let
A
,
B
be positive integers.
Let
x^{(A)}:=(x(1),\ldots,x(A))\in\mathcal{X}^{A}
denote a
|A|
-tuple
of elements from
\mathcal{X}
;
y^{(B)}
has a similar meaning.
For any pair of tuples
x^{(A)}
,
y^{(B)}
,
define the
sample average covering state

\sigma^{M}_{x^{(A)},y^{(B)}}:=(AB)^{-1}\sum_{a=1}^{A}\sum_{b=1}^{B}\frac{p^{XY}(x(a),y(b))}{q^{X}(x(a))q^{Y}(y(b))}\rho^{M}_{x(a),y(b)},

(35)

where the fraction term above represents the ‘change of measure’ from
the product probability distribution
q^{X}\times q^{Y}
to the
joint probability distribution
p^{XY}
.

Let
\bar{q}^{X^{A}}
,
\bar{q}^{Y^{B}}
be two probability distributions
on
\mathcal{X}^{A}
,
\mathcal{Y}^{B}
with the following properties:

1.

The marginal on any coordinate
\bar{q}^{X_{a}}
for any
a\in[A]
equals

q^{X}
;

2.

The marginal on any two coordinates
\bar{q}^{X_{a}X_{a^{\prime}}}
for any

a,a^{\prime}\in[A]
,
a\neq a^{\prime}
is independent of
a
,
a^{\prime}
; hence it
can be modelled as a distribution
\bar{q}^{XX^{\prime}}
;

3.

Similar properties as the two above hold for
\bar{q}^{Y^{B}}
.

For any
(x,y)\in\mathcal{X}\times\mathcal{Y}
, define the positive semidefinite
matrices

\begin{array}[]{c}\rho(X)^{M}_{xy}:=\sum_{y^{\prime}}\frac{\bar{q}^{Y^{\prime}|Y=y}(y^{\prime})p^{Y|X=x}(y^{\prime})}{q^{Y}(y^{\prime})}\rho^{M}_{xy^{\prime}},~\rho(Y)^{M}_{xy}:=\sum_{x^{\prime}}\frac{\bar{q}^{X^{\prime}|X=x}(x^{\prime})p^{X|Y=y}(x^{\prime})}{q^{X}(x^{\prime})}\rho^{M}_{x^{\prime}y},\\
\rho()^{M}_{xy}:=\sum_{x^{\prime}y^{\prime}}\frac{\bar{q}^{Y^{\prime}|Y=y}(y^{\prime})q^{X^{\prime}|X=x}(x^{\prime})p(x^{\prime},y^{\prime})}{q^{X}(x^{\prime})q^{Y}(y^{\prime})}\rho^{M}_{x^{\prime}y^{\prime}}.\end{array}

Definition 3

Let
0<\epsilon<1
.
For the two party classical quantum soft covering problem,
the consequences of loss of pairwise independence are said to hold up to
scale factors of
(\epsilon,f(\epsilon),g(\epsilon))
if there exist
probability distributions

p(X)^{XY}
,
p(Y)^{XY}
,
p()^{XY}
satisfying

\|p^{XY}-p(X)^{XY}\|_{1}<\epsilon,~~\|p^{XY}-p(Y)^{XY}\|_{1}<\epsilon,~~\|p^{XY}-p()^{XY}\|_{1}<\epsilon,

such that

\rho(X)^{XYM}\leq f(\epsilon)\rho^{XM,Y|X},~\rho(Y)^{XYM}\leq f(\epsilon)\rho^{YM,X|Y},~\rho()^{XYM}\leq(1+g(\epsilon))(p^{XY}\otimes\rho^{M}),

where

\begin{array}[]{c}\rho(X)^{XYM}:=\sum_{(x,y)\in\mathcal{X}\times\mathcal{Y}}p(X)^{XY}(x,y)|x,y\rangle\langle x,y|^{XY}\otimes\rho(X)^{M}_{xy},\\
\rho(Y)^{XYM}:=\sum_{(x,y)\in\mathcal{X}\times\mathcal{Y}}p(Y)^{XY}(x,y)|x,y\rangle\langle x,y|^{XY}\otimes\rho(Y)^{M}_{xy},\\
\rho()^{XYM}:=\sum_{(x,y)\in\mathcal{X}\times\mathcal{Y}}p()^{XY}(x,y)|x,y\rangle\langle x,y|^{XY}\otimes\rho()^{M}_{xy}.\end{array}

In this paper, we shall take

f(\epsilon)\leq 2^{\mathrm{polylog}(\epsilon^{-1})}
and

g(\epsilon)\leq\epsilon^{\Theta(1)}
.

We are now ready to give the statement of our smooth two party classical
quantum covering lemma under slight relaxation of pairwise independence.

Proposition 5

Consider the setting of the two party classical quantum soft covering
problem.
Suppose,

\displaystyle\log A

\displaystyle>

\displaystyle D^{\epsilon}_{\infty}(\rho^{XM}\|q^{X}\otimes\rho^{M})+\log f(e)+3+\log\epsilon^{-1/2},

\displaystyle\log B

\displaystyle>

\displaystyle D^{\epsilon}_{\infty}(\rho^{YM}\|q^{Y}\otimes\rho^{M})+\log f(e)+3+\log\epsilon^{-1/2},

\displaystyle\log A+\log B

\displaystyle>

\displaystyle D^{\epsilon}_{\infty}(\rho^{XYM}\|q^{X}\otimes q^{Y}\otimes\rho^{M})+1+\log\epsilon^{-1/2}.

Then,

\E_{x^{(A)},y^{(B)}}[\|\sigma^{M}_{x^{(A)},y^{(B)}}-\rho^{M}\|_{1}]<(2\sqrt{15\epsilon^{1/64}+\sqrt{40\epsilon^{1/32}+8e+g(e)}}+4e)(\Tr\rho).

where the expectation is taken over independent choices of tuples

x^{(A)}
,
y^{(B)}
from the distributions
\bar{q}^{X^{A}}
,

\bar{q}^{Y^{B}}
which satisfy the consequences of loss of
pairwise independence
up to scale factors of
(e,f(e),g(e))
.
Note that for the usual setting of
e=\epsilon
,

f(e)\leq 2^{\mathrm{polylog}(e^{-1})}
and

g(e)\leq e^{\Theta(1)}
, the right hand
sides
of the inequalities in the above rate region will have additive terms
of
\mathrm{polylog}(\epsilon^{-1})
and the covering error
will be bounded by
\epsilon^{\Theta(1)}
.

Remarks:

1. In the pairwise independent setting,
\bar{q}^{XX^{\prime}}=q^{X}\times q^{X^{\prime}}
,

\bar{q}^{YY^{\prime}}=q^{X}\times q^{Y^{\prime}}
. Hence,

\rho(X)^{XYM}=\rho^{XM,Y|X}
,

\rho(Y)^{XYM}=\rho^{YM,X|Y}
and

\rho()^{XYM}=p^{XY}\otimes\rho^{M}
. Thus,
\bar{q}^{XX^{\prime}}
,

\bar{q}^{YY^{\prime}}
satisfy the consequences of loss of pairwise independence
up to scale factors of
(0,1,0)
i.e. perfectly. The rate region and
covering error promised by Proposition
5

is essentially the same as that given by the smooth soft covering
lemma of
[
Sen24
]
. The main difference is that
the rate region of Proposition
5
is
given in terms of

D^{\epsilon}_{\infty}(\cdot\|\cdot)
versus
D^{\epsilon}_{2}(\cdot\|\cdot)

in
[
Sen24
]
.

2. Proposition
5
cannot be proved by the
telescoping technique. Consider a simplified setting where

p^{XY}=p^{X}\otimes p^{Y}
,
q^{X}=p^{X}
,
q^{Y}=p^{Y}
,

p^{XY}=p(X)^{XY}=p(Y)^{XY}=p()^{XY}
, but

q^{XX^{\prime}}\neq q^{X}\otimes q^{X^{\prime}}
,
q^{YY^{\prime}}\neq q^{Y}\otimes q^{Y^{\prime}}
.
In other words,
p^{XY}
is a tensor
product distribution, the sampling distributions
q^{X}
,
q^{Y}
are the
same as the marginals
p^{X}
,
p^{Y}
but there is a loss of pairwise
independence in sampling
(x(a))_{a}
as well as in sampling
(y(b))_{b}
;
nevertheless
p^{XY}
does not require perturbation in order to
satisfy the operator inequalities involving
f(e)
,
g(e)
.
Telescoping starts off by using the mapping

(x,y)\mapsto\rho^{{}^{\prime}M}_{xy}:=(\rho^{M}_{xy}-\rho^{M}_{x}-\rho^{M}_{y}+\rho^{M})

instead of using the original mapping

(x,y)\mapsto\rho^{M}_{xy}
.
The mean zero requirement of telescoping is the fact that

\forall(x,y):~~\E_{(x,x^{\prime})\sim p^{X}\times p^{X^{\prime}}}[\rho^{{}^{\prime}M}_{xy}\rho^{{}^{\prime}M}_{x^{\prime}y}]=\E_{(y,y^{\prime})\sim p^{Y}\times p^{Y^{\prime}}}[\rho^{{}^{\prime}M}_{xy}\rho^{{}^{\prime}M}_{xy^{\prime}}]=0.

However under the ‘slight breaking of pairwise independence’, we have to
evaluate the expectation under
(x,x^{\prime})\sim q^{XX^{\prime}}
, and similarly for

(y,y^{\prime})
. Since
q^{XX^{\prime}}\neq p^{X}\times p^{X^{\prime}}
, the mean zero requirement
fails. Replacing
\rho^{{}^{\prime}M}_{xy}
with other candidates like

(\rho^{M}_{xy}-\rho(X)^{M}_{xy}-\rho(Y)^{M}_{xy}+\rho()^{M}_{xy})

does not help either.

To prove Proposition
5
, we generally
follow the lines of the proof of Lemma
7
.
Several steps are simpler because
X
,
Y
are classical. In particular,
we do not need to flatten
q^{X}
,
q^{Y}
. Only the flattening superoperator

\mathcal{F}_{\rho}^{M\rightarrow L_{M}M}
is needed. Again, without loss of
generality, we assume that
\rho^{XYM}
is a normalised density matrix.

From the notion of intersection of classical probability distributions
defined in Equation
6
, we get

\|(p\cap p(X)\cap p(Y)\cap p())^{XY}-p^{XY}\|_{1}<3e.

Arguing along the lines of the initial part of the proof of
Proposition
2
and defining a set

\mathrm{Bad}:=\{(x,y):p^{XY}(x,y)>4(p\cap p(X)\cap p(Y)\cap p())^{XY}(x,y)\},

we see that there
is a subnormalised probability distribution
\hat{p}^{XY}
such that

\|\hat{p}^{XY}-p^{XY}\|_{1}<4e,\hat{p}^{XY}\leq p^{XY}<4\hat{p}^{XY},\hat{p}^{XY}\leq p(X)^{XY},\hat{p}^{XY}\leq p(Y)^{XY},\hat{p}^{XY}\leq p()^{XY},

(36)

where the inequality
p^{XY}<4\hat{p}^{XY}
above holds only
over
\mathrm{supp}(\hat{p}^{XY})
.
We can define the sample average covering state

\hat{\sigma}^{M}_{x^{(A)},y^{(B)}}
using
\hat{p}^{XY}
instead of

p^{XY}
as in Equation
35
in the natural fashion.
Similarly, we can define the new classical quantum control state

\hat{\rho}^{XYM}
.
Then for any
x^{(A)}
,
y^{(B)}
, using Equations
35
,

36
, we can easily see that

\|\hat{\sigma}^{M}_{x^{(A)},y^{(B)}}-\sigma^{M}_{x^{(A)},y^{(B)}}\|_{1}<4e.

So in order to prove Proposition
5
, it
suffices to prove

\E_{x^{(A)},y^{(B)}}[\|\hat{\sigma}^{M}_{x^{(A)},y^{(B)}}-\rho^{M}\|_{1}]<2\sqrt{15\epsilon^{1/64}+\sqrt{40\epsilon^{1/32}+8e+g(e)}}.

(37)

From the definitions of smooth
D^{\epsilon}_{\infty}(\cdot\|\cdot)
,
we have subnormalised classical quantum density matrices

\rho^{XYM}(3)
,
\rho^{XM}(1)
and
\rho^{YM}(2)
satisfying

\begin{array}[]{c}\|\rho^{XYM}-\rho^{XYM}(3)\|_{1}\leq\epsilon,~~\|\rho^{XM}-\rho^{XM}(1)\|_{1}\leq\epsilon,~~\|\rho^{YM}-\rho^{YM}(2)\|_{1}\leq\epsilon,\end{array}

such that

\begin{array}[]{rcl}\rho^{XYM}(3)&\leq&2^{D^{\epsilon}_{\infty}(\rho^{XYM}\|q^{X}\otimes q^{Y}\otimes\rho^{M})}(q^{X}\otimes q^{Y}\otimes\rho^{M}),\\
\rho^{XM}(1)&\leq&2^{D^{\epsilon}_{\infty}(\rho^{XM}\|q^{X}\otimes\rho^{M})}(q^{X}\otimes\rho^{M}),\\
\rho^{YM}(2)&\leq&2^{D^{\epsilon}_{\infty}(\rho(Y)^{YM}\|q^{Y}\otimes\rho^{M})}.\end{array}

(38)

Note that in general
\rho^{XM}(1)
,
\rho^{YM}(2)
have nothing to
do with the marginals of
\rho^{XYM}(3)
.
From Equation
36
and the triangle inequality,
we can write

\begin{array}[]{c}\|\hat{\rho}^{XYM}-\rho^{XYM}(3)\|_{1}\leq 4e+\epsilon,\|\hat{\rho}^{XM}-\rho^{XM}(1)\|_{1}\leq 4e+\epsilon,\|\hat{\rho}^{YM}-\rho^{YM}(2)\|_{1}\leq 4e+\epsilon.\end{array}

(39)

We can extend the classical quantum states
\rho^{XM}(1)
,
\rho^{YM}(2)

to classical quantum states
\rho^{XM,Y|X}(1)
,
\rho^{YM,X|Y}(2)
in
the natural fashion. Henceforth we will be working with the control
state
\hat{\rho}^{XYM}
and probability distribution
\hat{p}^{XY}
only.
So to ease the notation below, we rename
\hat{p}^{XY}
to
p^{XY}
,

\hat{\rho}^{XYM}
to
\rho^{XYM}
,
\hat{\sigma}^{M}_{x^{(A)},y^{(B)}}
to

\sigma^{M}_{x^{(A)},y^{(B)}}
. Under this simplified notation,
in order to prove Proposition
5
,
Equation
37
rewritten says that
it suffices to show

\E_{x^{(A)},y^{(B)}}[\|\sigma^{M}_{x^{(A)},y^{(B)}}-\rho^{M}\|_{1}]<2\sqrt{15\epsilon^{1/64}+\sqrt{40\epsilon^{1/32}+8e+g(e)}}.

(40)

Also, Equation
39
is rewritten as

\begin{array}[]{c}\|\rho^{XYM}-\rho^{XYM}(3)\|_{1}\leq 4e+\epsilon,\|\rho^{XM}-\rho^{XM}(1)\|_{1}\leq 4e+\epsilon,\|\rho^{YM}-\rho^{YM}(2)\|_{1}\leq 4e+\epsilon.\end{array}

(41)

Apply the flattening CPTP superoperator to get the normalised
density matrix

\rho^{XYL_{M}M}:=(\mathbb{I}^{XY}\otimes\mathcal{F}_{\rho}^{M\rightarrow L_{M}M})(\rho^{XYM}).

Define
\rho^{XYL_{M}M}(3)
,
\rho^{XL_{M}M}(1)
,

\rho^{YL_{M}M}(2)
,
\rho(X)^{XYL_{M}M}
,
\rho(Y)^{XYL_{M}M}
,

\rho()^{XYL_{M}M}
via flattening in the natural fashion.
Extend
\rho^{XL_{M}M}(1)
,
\rho^{YL_{M}M}(2)

to classical quantum states
\rho^{XL_{M}M,Y|X}(1)
,

\rho^{YL_{M}M,X|Y}(2)
in the natural fashion.
Then we use Equation
41
and conclude,

\begin{array}[]{c}\|\rho^{XYL_{M}M}-\rho^{XYL_{M}M}(3)\|_{1}<4e+\epsilon,\\
\|\rho^{XL_{M}M}-\rho^{XL_{M}M}(1)\|_{1}<4e+\epsilon,~~\|\rho^{YL_{M}M}-\rho^{YL_{M}M}(2)\|_{1}<4e+\epsilon,\end{array}

(42)

since a CPTP superoperator cannot increase the trace distance.

By Lemma
6
and Proposition
3
,
we have

(1+\delta)^{-2}\frac{\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{F^{\prime}_{\rho}}}{|F^{\prime}_{\rho}|}\leq\mathcal{F}_{\rho}^{M\rightarrow L_{M}M}(\rho^{M})\leq(1+\delta)\frac{\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{F^{\prime}_{\rho}}}{|F^{\prime}_{\rho}|}.

(43)

Because a CPTP superoperator respects the Löwner partial order on
Hermitian matrices, combining Equations
38
and

43
gives us

\begin{array}[]{rcl}\rho^{XYL_{M}M}(3)&\leq&(1+\delta)2^{D^{\epsilon}_{\infty}(\rho^{XYM}\|q^{X}\otimes q^{Y}\otimes\rho^{M})}(q^{X}\otimes q^{Y}\otimes(\frac{\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{F^{\prime}_{\rho}}}{|F^{\prime}_{\rho}|})),\\
\rho^{XL_{M}M}(1)&\leq&(1+\delta)2^{D^{\epsilon}_{\infty}(\rho^{XM}\|q^{X}\otimes\rho^{M})}(q^{X}\otimes(\frac{\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{F^{\prime}_{\rho}}}{|F^{\prime}_{\rho}|})),\\
\rho^{YL_{M}M}(2)&\leq&(1+\delta)2^{D^{\epsilon}_{\infty}(\rho^{YM}\|q^{Y}\otimes\rho^{M})}(q^{Y}\otimes(\frac{\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{F^{\prime}_{\rho}}}{|F^{\prime}_{\rho}|})).\end{array}

(44)

Applying Proposition
2
to
Equations
42
,
44
tells us that
there exists classical quantum orthogonal projectors

\displaystyle\Pi^{XYL_{M}M}(3)

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle\sum_{xy}|x,y\rangle\langle x,y|^{XY}\otimes\Pi^{L_{M}M}(3)_{xy},

\displaystyle\Pi^{XL_{M}M}(1)

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle\sum_{x}|x\rangle\langle x|^{X}\otimes\Pi^{L_{M}M}(1)_{x},

\displaystyle\Pi^{YL_{M}M}(2)

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle\sum_{y}|y\rangle\langle y|^{Y}\otimes\Pi^{L_{M}M}(2)_{y},

such that

\begin{array}[]{rcl}\Tr[\Pi^{XYL_{M}M}(3)\rho^{XYL_{M}M}]&\geq&1-\sqrt{4e+\epsilon},\\
\Tr[\Pi^{XL_{M}M}(1)\rho^{XL_{M}M}]&\geq&1-\sqrt{4e+\epsilon},\\
\Tr[\Pi^{YL_{M}M}(2)\rho^{YL_{M}M}]&\geq&1-\sqrt{4e+\epsilon},\\
\Pi^{XYL_{M}M}(3)\rho^{XYL_{M}M}&=&\rho^{XYL_{M}M}\Pi^{XYL_{M}M}(3),\\
\Pi^{XL_{M}M}(1)\rho^{XL_{M}M}&=&\rho^{XL_{M}M}\Pi^{XL_{M}M}(1),\\
\Pi^{YL_{M}M}(2)\rho^{YL_{M}M}&=&\rho^{YL_{M}M}\Pi^{YL_{M}M}(2),\\
\forall(x,y):\frac{p(x,y)}{q(x)q(y)}\|\Pi^{L_{M}M}_{xy}(3)\,\rho^{L_{M}M}_{xy}\|_{\infty}&\leq&\frac{(1+\delta)(1+2\sqrt{4e+\epsilon})2^{D^{\epsilon}_{\infty}(\rho^{XYM}\|q^{X}\otimes q^{Y}\otimes\rho^{M})}}{|F^{\prime}_{\rho}|},\\
\forall x:\frac{p(x)}{q(x)}\|\Pi^{L_{M}M}_{x}(1)\,\rho^{L_{M}M}_{x}\|_{\infty}&\leq&\frac{(1+\delta)(1+2\sqrt{4e+\epsilon})2^{D^{\epsilon}_{\infty}(\rho^{XM}\|q^{X}\otimes\rho^{M})}}{|F^{\prime}_{\rho}|},\\
\forall y:\frac{p(y)}{q(y)}\|\Pi^{L_{M}M}_{y}(2)\,\rho^{L_{M}M}_{y}\|_{\infty}&\leq&\frac{(1+\delta)(1+2\sqrt{4e+\epsilon})2^{D^{\epsilon}_{\infty}(\rho^{YM}\|q^{Y}\otimes\rho^{M})}}{|F^{\prime}_{\rho}|}.\end{array}

(45)

Define the
flattened sample average covering state

\sigma^{L_{M}M}_{x^{(A)},y^{(B)}}:=(AB)^{-1}\sum_{a=1}^{A}\sum_{b=1}^{B}\frac{p^{XY}(x(a),y(b))}{q^{X}(x(a))q^{Y}(y(b))}\rho^{L_{M}M}_{x(a),y(b)},

Proposition
4
and
Fact
2
together with concavity of square
root imply that

\begin{array}[]{rcl}\E_{x^{(A)},y^{(B)}}[\|\sigma^{M}_{x^{(A)},y^{(B)}}-\rho^{M}\|_{1}]&\leq&2\E_{x^{(A)},y^{(B)}}[\sqrt{\|\sigma^{L_{M}M}_{x^{(A)},y^{(B)}}-\rho^{L_{M}M}\|_{1}}]\\
&\leq&2\sqrt{\E_{x^{(A)},y^{(B)}}[\|\sigma^{L_{M}M}_{x^{(A)},y^{(B)}}-\rho^{L_{M}M}\|_{1}]},\end{array}

(46)

where the expectation is taken over independent choices of tuples

x^{(A)}
,
y^{(B)}
from the distributions
\bar{q}^{X^{A}}
,

\bar{q}^{Y^{B}}
.
We note that
\delta
can be taken as small as we please
at the expense of increasing
|F^{\prime}_{\rho}|
. Essentially,

\delta
can be treated as a free parameter. We will set

\delta:=\sqrt{\epsilon}/3
.

To make the
notation lighter, henceforth we will always work with the flattened
states and the Hilbert space
L_{M}M
. Hence from
now on we redefine
M\equiv L_{M}M
. Under this redefinition, we restate
Equations
45
,
43
, and
the consequences of Equation
36
as
Equation
47
below. That is, we can assume that there
are classical quantum orthogonal projectors

\Pi^{XYM}(3)
,
\Pi^{XM}(1)
and
\Pi^{YM}(2)
such that

\begin{array}[]{rcl}\Tr[\Pi^{XYM}(3)\rho^{XYM}]&\geq&1-\sqrt{\epsilon}-2\sqrt{e},\\
\Tr[\Pi^{XM}(1)\rho^{XM}]&\geq&1-\sqrt{\epsilon}-2\sqrt{e},\\
\Tr[\Pi^{YM}(2)\rho^{YM}]&\geq&1-\sqrt{\epsilon}-2\sqrt{e},\\
\Pi^{XYM}(3)\rho^{XYM}&=&\rho^{XYM}\Pi^{XYM}(3),\\
\Pi^{XM}(1)\rho^{XM}&=&\rho^{XM}\Pi^{XM}(1),\\
\Pi^{YM}(2)\rho^{YM}&=&\rho^{YM}\Pi^{YM}(2),\\
\forall(x,y)\in\mathrm{supp}(p^{XY}):\rho(X)^{M}_{xy}&\leq&4f(e)\rho^{M}_{x},\\
\forall(x,y)\in\mathrm{supp}(p^{XY}):\rho(Y)^{M}_{xy}&\leq&4f(e)\rho^{M}_{y},\\
\forall(x,y)\in\mathrm{supp}(p^{XY}):\rho()^{M}_{xy}&\leq&(1+g(e))\rho^{M},\\
\forall(x,y):\frac{p(x,y)}{q(x)q(y)}\|\Pi^{M}_{xy}(3)\,\rho^{M}_{xy}\|_{\infty}&\leq&\frac{(1+4\sqrt{e}+2\sqrt{\epsilon})2^{D^{\epsilon}_{\infty}(\rho^{XYM}\|q^{X}\otimes q^{Y}\otimes\rho^{M})}}{|F^{\prime}_{\rho}|},\\
\forall x:\frac{p(x)}{q(x)}\|\Pi^{M}_{x}(1)\,\rho^{M}_{x}\|_{\infty}&\leq&\frac{(1+4\sqrt{e}+2\sqrt{\epsilon})2^{D^{\epsilon}_{\infty}(\rho^{XM}\|q^{X}\otimes\rho^{M})}}{|F^{\prime}_{\rho}|},\\
\forall y:\frac{p(y)}{q(y)}\|\Pi^{M}_{y}(2)\,\rho^{M}_{y}\|_{\infty}&\leq&\frac{(1+4\sqrt{e}+2\sqrt{\epsilon})2^{D^{\epsilon}_{\infty}(\rho^{YM}\|q^{Y}\otimes\rho^{M})}}{|F^{\prime}_{\rho}|},\\
F^{\prime}_{\rho}&\leq&M,\\
(1-\sqrt{\epsilon})\frac{\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{F^{\prime}_{\rho}}}{|F^{\prime}_{\rho}|}&\leq&\rho^{M}\;\leq\;(1+\sqrt{\epsilon})\frac{\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{F^{\prime}_{\rho}}}{|F^{\prime}_{\rho}|}.\end{array}

(47)

To finish the proof of Proposition
5
, we
only need to show

\E_{x^{(A)},y^{(B)}}[\|\sigma^{M}_{x^{(A)},y^{(B)}}-\rho^{M}\|_{1}]\leq 15\epsilon^{1/64}+\sqrt{40\epsilon^{1/32}+8e+g(e)}.

(48)

because of Equation
46
.

In order to prove the above inequality, we augment
X
to
L_{X}X
,

Y
to
L_{Y}Y
as in Section
3.4
.
Recall that the new spaces
L_{X}
,
L_{Y}
required for augmentation
have the same dimension
L
. The value of
L
will be chosen later,
and it will be sufficiently large for our later purposes. Keeping
augmentation in mind, we define

q^{L_{X}X}:=\frac{\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{L_{X}}}{L}\otimes q^{X},~q^{L_{Y}Y}:=\frac{\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{L_{Y}}}{L}\otimes q^{Y},~~\rho^{L_{X}XL_{Y}YM}:=\frac{\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{L_{X}}}{L}\otimes\frac{\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{L_{Y}}}{L}\otimes\rho^{XYM}.

As in Section
3.4
, we enlarge the
Hilbert space
M
into a larger space
\hat{M}
. Because of this containment,
the state

\rho^{L_{X}XL_{Y}Y\hat{M}}
is identical to
\rho^{L_{X}XL_{Y}YM}
; which
notation we use depends on the application.
Define tuples
(l_{x}x)^{(A)}
,
(l_{y}y)^{(B)}
in the natural fashion.
Define the normalised
state
\hat{\rho}^{L_{X}XL_{Y}Y\hat{M}}
and subnormalised state

\hat{\hat{\rho}}^{L_{X}XL_{Y}Y\hat{M}}
as in Equation
9
via
the classical quantum projectors
\Pi^{XYM}(3)
,

\Pi^{XYM}(2):=\Pi^{YM}(2)\otimes\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{X}
,

\Pi^{XYM}(1):=\Pi^{XM}(1)\otimes\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{Y}
.
Observe that the states
\hat{\rho}^{L_{X}XL_{Y}Y\hat{M}}
,

\hat{\hat{\rho}}^{L_{X}XL_{Y}Y\hat{M}}
, and
the approximate intersection projector

\hat{\Pi}^{L_{X}XL_{Y}Y\hat{M}}

of Equation
8
are now classical quantum.
Thus we can write

\begin{array}[]{rcl}\hat{\rho}^{L_{X}XL_{Y}Y\hat{M}}&:=&L^{-2}\sum_{l_{x},x,l_{y},y}p^{XY}(x,y)|l_{x},x,l_{y},y\rangle\langle l_{x},x,l_{y},y|^{L_{X}XL_{Y}Y}\otimes\hat{\rho}^{\hat{M}}_{l_{x}xl_{y}y},\\
\hat{\rho}^{\hat{M}}_{l_{x}xl_{y}y}&=&T^{M\rightarrow\hat{M}}_{l_{x},l_{y},\epsilon^{1/4}}(\rho^{M}_{xy}),\\
\hat{\hat{\rho}}^{L_{X}XL_{Y}Y\hat{M}}&:=&L^{-2}\sum_{l_{x},x,l_{y},y}p^{XY}(x,y)|l_{x},x,l_{y},y\rangle\langle l_{x},x,l_{y},y|^{L_{X}XL_{Y}Y}\otimes\hat{\hat{\rho}}^{\hat{M}}_{l_{x}xl_{y}y},\\
\forall i\in[3]:\hat{\Pi}^{XYM}(i)&:=&\sum_{x,y}|x,y\rangle\langle x,y|^{XY}\otimes\Pi^{M}_{xy}(i),\\
\hat{\Pi}^{L_{X}XL_{Y}Y\hat{M}}&:=&\sum_{l_{x},x,l_{y},y}|l_{x},x,l_{y},y\rangle\langle l_{x},x,l_{y},y|^{L_{X}XL_{Y}Y}\otimes\hat{\Pi}^{\hat{M}}_{l_{x}xl_{y}y},\\
\hat{\Pi}^{\hat{M}}_{l_{x}xl_{y}y}&=&\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{\hat{M}}-\spanning\{T^{M\rightarrow\hat{M}}_{l_{x},\epsilon^{1/4}}((\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{M}-\Pi^{M}_{x}(1))),T^{M\rightarrow\hat{M}}_{l_{y},\epsilon^{1/4}}((\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{M}-\Pi^{M}_{y}(2))),\\
&&~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~T^{M\rightarrow\hat{M}}_{l_{x},l_{y},\epsilon^{1/4}}((\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{M}-\Pi^{M}_{xy}(3)))\},\\
\hat{\hat{\rho}}^{\hat{M}}_{l_{x}xl_{y}y}&=&\hat{\Pi}^{\hat{M}}_{l_{x}xl_{y}y}\circ\rho^{\hat{M}}_{xy}.\end{array}

(49)

Define

\begin{array}[]{rcl}\sigma^{\hat{M}}_{(l_{x}x)^{(A)},(l_{y}y)^{(B)}}&:=&\sigma^{M}_{(l_{x}x)^{(A)},(l_{y}y)^{(B)}}\;=\;\sigma^{M}_{x^{(A)},y^{(B)}}\\
&=&(AB)^{-1}\sum_{a=1}^{A}\sum_{b=1}^{B}\frac{p^{XY}(x(a),y(b))}{q^{X}(x(a))q^{Y}(y(b))}\rho^{M}_{x(a),y(b)},\\
\hat{\sigma}^{\hat{M}}_{(l_{x}x)^{(A)},(l_{y}y)^{(B)}}&:=&(AB)^{-1}\sum_{a=1}^{A}\sum_{b=1}^{B}\frac{p^{XY}(x(a),y(b))}{q^{X}(x(a))q^{Y}(y(b))}\hat{\rho}^{\hat{M}}_{(l_{x}x)(a),(l_{y}y)(b)},\\
\hat{\hat{\sigma}}^{\hat{M}}_{(l_{x}x)^{(A)},(l_{y}y)^{(B)}}&:=&(AB)^{-1}\sum_{a=1}^{A}\sum_{b=1}^{B}\frac{p^{XY}(x(a),y(b))}{q^{X}(x(a))q^{Y}(y(b))}\hat{\hat{\rho}}^{\hat{M}}_{(l_{x}x)(a),(l_{y}y)(b)}.\end{array}

(50)

Observe that

\begin{array}[]{c}\forall((l_{x}x)^{(A)},(l_{y}y)^{(B)}):\mathrm{supp}(\sigma^{\hat{M}}_{(l_{x}x)^{(A)},(l_{y}y)^{(B)}})\leq F^{\prime}_{\rho}\leq M,\\
\|\rho^{XYM}\|_{1}=\|\rho^{M}\|_{1}=\|p^{XY}\|_{1}\geq 1-4e.\end{array}

(51)

Define the probability distributions

\bar{q}^{(L_{X}X)^{A}}:=(\frac{\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{L_{X}}}{L})^{\otimes A}\otimes\bar{q}^{X^{A}},~\bar{q}^{(L_{Y}Y)^{B}}:=(\frac{\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{L_{Y}}}{L})^{\otimes B}\otimes\bar{q}^{Y^{B}}.

Observe that

\displaystyle\E_{(l_{x}x)^{(A)},(l_{y}y)^{(B)}}[\|\sigma^{\hat{M}}_{(l_{x}x)^{(A)},(l_{y}y)^{(B)}}-\rho^{\hat{M}}\|_{1}]

\displaystyle\equiv

\displaystyle\E_{(l_{x}x)^{(A)},(l_{y}y)^{(B)}}[\|\sigma^{M}_{(l_{x}x)^{(A)},(l_{y}y)^{(B)}}-\rho^{M}\|_{1}]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\E_{x^{(A)},y^{(B)}}[\|\sigma^{M}_{x^{(A)},y^{(B)}}-\rho^{M}\|_{1}],

where the expectation is taken over independent choices of tuples

(l_{x}x)^{(A)}
,
(l_{y}y)^{(B)}
from the distributions

\bar{q}^{(L_{X}X)^{A}}
,
\bar{q}^{(L_{Y}Y)^{B}}
.
So to prove Proposition
5
, we just need to
show that

\E_{(l_{x}x)^{(A)},(l_{y}y)^{(B)}}[\|\sigma^{\hat{M}}_{(l_{x}x)^{(A)},(l_{y}y)^{(B)}}-\rho^{\hat{M}}\|_{1}]\leq 15\epsilon^{1/64}+\sqrt{40\epsilon^{1/32}+8e+g(e)}.

(52)

because of Equations
48
,
50
.

We now prove four lemmas which are crucially required in the following
arguments, leading to the proof of
Proposition
5
.

Lemma 14

\displaystyle L^{-2}\sum_{l_{x},x,l_{y},y}q^{X}(x)q^{Y}(y)\Tr\left[\left(\frac{p^{XY}(x,y)}{q^{X}(x)q^{Y}(y)}\hat{\rho}^{\hat{M}}_{l_{x}xl_{y}y}\right)\left(\frac{p^{XY}(x,y)}{q^{X}(x)q^{Y}(y)}\hat{\hat{\rho}}^{\hat{M}}_{l_{x}xl_{y}y}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{(1+4\sqrt{e}+2\sqrt{\epsilon})2^{D^{\epsilon}_{\infty}(\rho^{XYM}\|q^{X}\otimes q^{Y}\otimes\rho^{M})}}{|F^{\prime}_{\rho}|}.~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~

Proof:

Using Equations
49
,
47
and
arguing as in the proof of Lemma
9
, we get

\displaystyle L^{-2}\sum_{l_{x},x,l_{y},y}q^{X}(x)q^{Y}(y)\Tr\left[\left(\frac{p^{XY}(x,y)}{q^{X}(x)q^{Y}(y)}\hat{\rho}^{\hat{M}}_{l_{x}xl_{y}y}\right)\left(\frac{p^{XY}(x,y)}{q^{X}(x)q^{Y}(y)}\hat{\hat{\rho}}^{\hat{M}}_{l_{x}xl_{y}y}\right)\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle L^{-2}\sum_{l_{x},x,l_{y},y}\Tr\left[\left(\frac{p^{XY}(x,y)}{q^{X}(x)q^{Y}(y)}\hat{\rho}^{\hat{M}}_{l_{x}xl_{y}y}\right)(p^{XY}(x,y)\hat{\Pi}^{\hat{M}}_{l_{x}xl_{y}y}\circ\rho^{\hat{M}}_{xy})\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle L^{-2}\sum_{l_{x},x,l_{y},y}\Tr\left[\left(\frac{p^{XY}(x,y)}{q^{X}(x)q^{Y}(y)}T^{M\rightarrow\hat{M}}_{l_{x},l_{y},\epsilon^{1/4}}(\rho^{M}_{xy})\right)\right.

\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~\left.(p^{XY}(x,y)((\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{\hat{M}}-T^{M\rightarrow\hat{M}}_{l_{x},l_{y},\epsilon^{1/4}}((\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{M}-\Pi^{M}_{xy}(3))))\circ(\hat{\Pi}^{\hat{M}}_{l_{x}xl_{y}y}\circ\rho^{\hat{M}}_{xy}))\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle L^{-2}\sum_{l_{x},x,l_{y},y}\Tr\left[\left(\frac{p^{XY}(x,y)}{q^{X}(x)q^{Y}(y)}(T^{M\rightarrow\hat{M}}_{l_{x},l_{y},\epsilon^{1/4}}(\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{M}))\circ(T^{M\rightarrow\hat{M}}_{l_{x},l_{y},\epsilon^{1/4}}(\rho^{M}_{xy}))\right)\right.

\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~\left.(p^{XY}(x,y)((\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{\hat{M}}-T^{M\rightarrow\hat{M}}_{l_{x},l_{y},\epsilon^{1/4}}((\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{M}-\Pi^{M}_{xy}(3))))\circ\hat{\hat{\rho}}^{\hat{M}}_{l_{x}xl_{y}y}))\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle L^{-2}\sum_{l_{x},x,l_{y},y}\Tr\left[\left(\frac{p^{XY}(x,y)}{q^{X}(x)q^{Y}(y)}T^{M\rightarrow\hat{M}}_{l_{x},l_{y},\epsilon^{1/4}}(\rho^{M}_{xy})\right)\right.

\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~\left.(p^{XY}(x,y)((T^{M\rightarrow\hat{M}}_{l_{x},l_{y},\epsilon^{1/4}}(\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{M})-T^{M\rightarrow\hat{M}}_{l_{x},l_{y},\epsilon^{1/4}}((\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{M}-\Pi^{M}_{xy}(3))))\circ\hat{\hat{\rho}}^{\hat{M}}_{l_{x}xl_{y}y}))\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle L^{-2}\sum_{l_{x},x,l_{y},y}\Tr\left[\left(\frac{p^{XY}(x,y)}{q^{X}(x)q^{Y}(y)}T^{M\rightarrow\hat{M}}_{l_{x},l_{y},\epsilon^{1/4}}(\rho^{M}_{xy})\right)(p^{XY}(x,y)((T^{M\rightarrow\hat{M}}_{l_{x},l_{y},\epsilon^{1/4}}(\Pi^{M}_{xy}(3)))\circ\hat{\hat{\rho}}^{\hat{M}}_{l_{x}xl_{y}y}))\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle L^{-2}\sum_{l_{x},x,l_{y},y}\Tr\left[\left(\frac{p^{XY}(x,y)}{q^{X}(x)q^{Y}(y)}T^{M\rightarrow\hat{M}}_{l_{x},l_{y},\epsilon^{1/4}}(\Pi^{M}_{xy}(3)\circ\rho^{M}_{xy})\right)(p^{XY}(x,y)\hat{\hat{\rho}}^{\hat{M}}_{l_{x}xl_{y}y})\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle L^{-2}\sum_{l_{x},x,l_{y},y}\frac{p^{XY}(x,y)}{q^{X}(x)q^{Y}(y)}\|T^{M\rightarrow\hat{M}}_{l_{x},l_{y},\epsilon^{1/4}}(\Pi^{M}_{xy}(3)\circ\rho^{M}_{xy})\|_{\infty}\cdot(p^{XY}(x,y)\|\hat{\hat{\rho}}^{\hat{M}}_{l_{x}xl_{y}y}\|_{1})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle L^{-2}\sum_{l_{x},x,l_{y},y}\frac{p^{XY}(x,y)}{q^{X}(x)q^{Y}(y)}\|\Pi^{M}_{xy}(3)\circ\rho^{M}_{xy})\|_{\infty}\cdot(p^{XY}(x,y)\|\hat{\hat{\rho}}^{\hat{M}}_{l_{x}xl_{y}y}\|_{1})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle L^{-2}\sum_{l_{x},x,l_{y},y}\frac{(1+4\sqrt{e}+2\sqrt{\epsilon})2^{D^{\epsilon}_{\infty}(\rho^{XYM}\|q^{X}\otimes q^{Y}\otimes\rho^{M})}}{|F^{\prime}_{\rho}|}p^{XY}(x,y)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{(1+4\sqrt{e}+2\sqrt{\epsilon})2^{D^{\epsilon}_{\infty}(\rho^{XYM}\|q^{X}\otimes q^{Y}\otimes\rho^{M})}}{|F^{\prime}_{\rho}|}.

The proof of the lemma is now complete.

\square

Lemma 15

\displaystyle L^{-3}\sum_{l_{x},x,l_{y},y,l^{\prime}_{y},y^{\prime}}q^{X}(x)\bar{q}^{YY^{\prime}}(y,y^{\prime})\Tr\left[\left(\frac{p^{XY}(x,y^{\prime})}{q^{X}(x)q^{Y}(y^{\prime})}\hat{\rho}^{\hat{M}}_{l_{x}xl^{\prime}_{y}y^{\prime}}\right)\left(\frac{p^{XY}(x,y)}{q^{X}(x)q^{Y}(y)}\hat{\hat{\rho}}^{\hat{M}}_{l_{x}xl_{y}y}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 4\epsilon^{1/8}L^{-1/2}\sum_{x,y}p^{XY}(x,y)\frac{p^{X}(x)}{q^{X}(x)}\|\rho(X)^{M}_{xy}\|_{1}+\frac{4(1+4\sqrt{e}+2\sqrt{\epsilon})f(e)2^{D^{\epsilon}_{\infty}(\rho^{XM}\|q^{X}\otimes\rho^{M})}}{|F^{\prime}_{\rho}|}.

Proof:

Using Equations
49
,
47
and
Lemma
4
, and
arguing as in the proof of Lemma
10
, we get

\displaystyle L^{-3}\sum_{l_{x},x,l_{y},y,l^{\prime}_{y},y^{\prime}}q^{X}(x)\bar{q}^{YY^{\prime}}(y,y^{\prime})\Tr\left[\left(\frac{p^{XY}(x,y^{\prime})}{q^{X}(x)q^{Y}(y^{\prime})}\hat{\rho}^{\hat{M}}_{l_{x}xl^{\prime}_{y}y^{\prime}}\right)\left(\frac{p^{XY}(x,y)}{q^{X}(x)q^{Y}(y)}\hat{\hat{\rho}}^{\hat{M}}_{l_{x}xl_{y}y}\right)\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle L^{-3}\sum_{l_{x},x,l_{y},y,l^{\prime}_{y},y^{\prime}}\frac{p^{X}(x)}{q^{X}(x)}\cdot\frac{p^{Y|X=x}(y^{\prime})\bar{q}^{Y^{\prime}|Y=y}(y^{\prime})}{q^{Y}(y^{\prime})}\Tr\left[\hat{\rho}^{\hat{M}}_{l_{x}xl^{\prime}_{y}y^{\prime}}(p^{XY}(x,y)\hat{\hat{\rho}}^{\hat{M}}_{l_{x}xl_{y}y})\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle L^{-3}\sum_{l_{x},x,l_{y},y,l^{\prime}_{y},y^{\prime}}\frac{p^{X}(x)}{q^{X}(x)}\cdot\frac{p^{Y|X=x}(y^{\prime})\bar{q}^{Y^{\prime}|Y=y}(y^{\prime})}{q^{Y}(y^{\prime})}\Tr\left[(T^{M\rightarrow\hat{M}}_{l_{x},l^{\prime}_{y},\epsilon^{1/4}}(\rho^{M}_{xy^{\prime}}))(p^{XY}(x,y)\hat{\hat{\rho}}^{\hat{M}}_{l_{x}xl_{y}y})\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle L^{-2}\sum_{l_{x},x,l_{y},y}\frac{p^{X}(x)}{q^{X}(x)}\Tr\left[\left(L^{-1}\sum_{l^{\prime}_{y}y^{\prime}}\frac{p^{Y|X=x}(y^{\prime})\bar{q}^{Y^{\prime}|Y=y}(y^{\prime})}{q^{Y}(y^{\prime})}T^{M\rightarrow\hat{M}}_{l_{x},l^{\prime}_{y},\epsilon^{1/4}}(\rho^{M}_{xy^{\prime}})\right)(p^{XY}(x,y)\hat{\hat{\rho}}^{\hat{M}}_{l_{x}xl_{y}y})\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle L^{-2}\sum_{l_{x},x,l_{y},y}\frac{p^{X}(x)}{q^{X}(x)}\Tr\left[\left(\left(L^{-1}\sum_{l^{\prime}_{y}}T^{M\rightarrow\hat{M}}_{l_{x},l^{\prime}_{y},\epsilon^{1/4}}\left(\sum_{y^{\prime}}\frac{p^{Y|X=x}(y^{\prime})\bar{q}^{Y^{\prime}|Y=y}(y^{\prime})}{q^{Y}(y^{\prime})}\rho^{M}_{xy^{\prime}}\right)\right)\right.\right.

\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~\left.\left.{}-T^{M\rightarrow\hat{M}}_{l_{x},\epsilon^{1/4}}\left(\sum_{y^{\prime}}\frac{p^{Y|X=x}(y^{\prime})\bar{q}^{Y^{\prime}|Y=y}(y^{\prime})}{q^{Y}(y^{\prime})}\rho^{M}_{xy^{\prime}}\right)\right)(p^{XY}(x,y)\hat{\hat{\rho}}^{\hat{M}}_{l_{x}xl_{y}y})\right]

\displaystyle{}+L^{-2}\sum_{l_{x},x,l_{y},y}\frac{p^{X}(x)}{q^{X}(x)}\Tr\left[\left(T^{M\rightarrow\hat{M}}_{l_{x},\epsilon^{1/4}}\left(\sum_{y^{\prime}}\frac{p^{Y|X=x}(y^{\prime})\bar{q}^{Y^{\prime}|Y=y}(y^{\prime})}{q^{Y}(y^{\prime})}\rho^{M}_{xy^{\prime}}\right)\right)(p^{XY}(x,y)\hat{\hat{\rho}}^{\hat{M}}_{l_{x}xl_{y}y})\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle L^{-2}\sum_{l_{x},x,l_{y},y}\frac{p^{X}(x)}{q^{X}(x)}\left\|\left(L^{-1}\sum_{l^{\prime}_{y}}T^{M\rightarrow\hat{M}}_{l_{x},l^{\prime}_{y},\epsilon^{1/4}}(\rho(X)^{M}_{xy})\right)-T^{M\rightarrow\hat{M}}_{l_{x},\epsilon^{1/4}}(\rho(X)^{M}_{xy})\right\|_{\infty}\cdot(p^{XY}(x,y)\|\hat{\hat{\rho}}^{\hat{M}}_{l_{x}xl_{y}y}\|_{1})

\displaystyle{}+L^{-2}\sum_{l_{x},x,l_{y},y}\frac{p^{X}(x)}{q^{X}(x)}\Tr\left[(T^{M\rightarrow\hat{M}}_{l_{x},\epsilon^{1/4}}(\rho(X)^{M}_{xy}))(p^{XY}(x,y)\hat{\hat{\rho}}^{\hat{M}}_{l_{x}xl_{y}y})\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 4\epsilon^{1/8}L^{-5/2}\sum_{l_{x},x,l_{y},y}p^{XY}(x,y)\frac{p^{X}(x)}{q^{X}(x)}\|\rho(X)^{M}_{xy}\|_{1}

\displaystyle~~~{}+L^{-2}\sum_{l_{x},x,l_{y},y}\frac{p^{X}(x)}{q^{X}(x)}\Tr\left[(T^{M\rightarrow\hat{M}}_{l_{x},\epsilon^{1/4}}(\rho(X)^{M}_{xy}))(p^{XY}(x,y)(\hat{\Pi}^{\hat{M}}_{l_{x}xl_{y}y}\circ\rho^{\hat{M}}_{xy}))\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 4\epsilon^{1/8}L^{-1/2}\sum_{x,y}p^{XY}(x,y)\frac{p^{X}(x)}{q^{X}(x)}\|\rho(X)^{M}_{xy}\|_{1}

\displaystyle~~~{}+L^{-2}\sum_{l_{x},x,l_{y},y}p^{XY}(x,y)\cdot\frac{p^{X}(x)}{q^{X}(x)}

\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~\Tr\left[T^{M\rightarrow\hat{M}}_{l_{x},\epsilon^{1/4}}(\rho(X)^{M}_{xy})((\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{\hat{M}}-T^{M\rightarrow\hat{M}}_{l_{x},\epsilon^{1/4}}((\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{M}-\Pi^{M}_{x}(1))))\circ(\hat{\Pi}^{\hat{M}}_{l_{x}xl_{y}y}\circ\rho^{\hat{M}}_{xy}))\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 4\epsilon^{1/8}L^{-1/2}\sum_{x,y}p^{XY}(x,y)\frac{p^{X}(x)}{q^{X}(x)}\|\rho(X)^{M}_{xy}\|_{1}

\displaystyle~~~{}+L^{-2}\sum_{l_{x},x,l_{y},y}p^{XY}(x,y)\cdot\frac{p^{X}(x)}{q^{X}(x)}

\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~\Tr[((T^{M\rightarrow\hat{M}}_{l_{x},\epsilon^{1/4}}(\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{M}))\circ(T^{M\rightarrow\hat{M}}_{l_{x},\epsilon^{1/4}}(\rho(X)^{M}_{xy})))

\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~((\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{\hat{M}}-T^{M\rightarrow\hat{M}}_{l_{x},\epsilon^{1/4}}((\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{M}-\Pi^{M}_{x}(1))))\circ\hat{\hat{\rho}}^{\hat{M}}_{l_{x}xl_{y}y})]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 4\epsilon^{1/8}L^{-1/2}\sum_{x,y}p^{XY}(x,y)\frac{p^{X}(x)}{q^{X}(x)}\|\rho(X)^{M}_{xy}\|_{1}

\displaystyle~~~{}+L^{-2}\sum_{l_{x},x,l_{y},y}p^{XY}(x,y)\cdot\frac{p^{X}(x)}{q^{X}(x)}

\displaystyle~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~\Tr[(T^{M\rightarrow\hat{M}}_{l_{x},\epsilon^{1/4}}(\rho(X)^{M}_{xy}))((T^{M\rightarrow\hat{M}}_{l_{x},\epsilon^{1/4}}(\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{M})-T^{M\rightarrow\hat{M}}_{l_{x},\epsilon^{1/4}}((\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{M}-\Pi^{M}_{x}(1))))\circ\hat{\hat{\rho}}^{\hat{M}}_{l_{x}xl_{y}y})]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 4\epsilon^{1/8}L^{-1/2}\sum_{x,y}p^{XY}(x,y)\frac{p^{X}(x)}{q^{X}(x)}\|\rho(X)^{M}_{xy}\|_{1}

\displaystyle~~~{}+L^{-2}\sum_{l_{x},x,l_{y},y}p^{XY}(x,y)\cdot\frac{p^{X}(x)}{q^{X}(x)}\Tr[(T^{M\rightarrow\hat{M}}_{l_{x},\epsilon^{1/4}}(\rho(X)^{M}_{xy}))((T^{M\rightarrow\hat{M}}_{l_{x},\epsilon^{1/4}}(\Pi^{M}_{x}(1)))\circ\hat{\hat{\rho}}^{\hat{M}}_{l_{x}xl_{y}y})]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 4\epsilon^{1/8}L^{-1/2}\sum_{x,y}p^{XY}(x,y)\frac{p^{X}(x)}{q^{X}(x)}\|\rho(X)^{M}_{xy}\|_{1}

\displaystyle~~~{}+L^{-2}\sum_{l_{x},x,l_{y},y}p^{XY}(x,y)\cdot\frac{p^{X}(x)}{q^{X}(x)}\Tr[(T^{M\rightarrow\hat{M}}_{l_{x},\epsilon^{1/4}}(\Pi^{M}_{x}(1)\circ\rho(X)^{M}_{xy}))\hat{\hat{\rho}}^{\hat{M}}_{l_{x}xl_{y}y})]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 4\epsilon^{1/8}L^{-1/2}\sum_{x,y}p^{XY}(x,y)\frac{p^{X}(x)}{q^{X}(x)}\|\rho(X)^{M}_{xy}\|_{1}

\displaystyle~~~{}+4f(e)L^{-2}\sum_{l_{x},x,l_{y},y}p^{XY}(x,y)\cdot\frac{p^{X}(x)}{q^{X}(x)}\Tr[(T^{M\rightarrow\hat{M}}_{l_{x},\epsilon^{1/4}}(\Pi^{M}_{x}(1)\circ\rho^{M}_{x}))\hat{\hat{\rho}}^{\hat{M}}_{l_{x}xl_{y}y})]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 4\epsilon^{1/8}L^{-1/2}\sum_{x,y}p^{XY}(x,y)\frac{p^{X}(x)}{q^{X}(x)}\|\rho(X)^{M}_{xy}\|_{1}

\displaystyle~~~{}+4f(e)L^{-2}\sum_{l_{x},x,l_{y},y}p^{XY}(x,y)\cdot\frac{p^{X}(x)}{q^{X}(x)}\|T^{M\rightarrow\hat{M}}_{l_{x},\epsilon^{1/4}}(\Pi^{M}_{x}(1)\circ\rho^{M}_{x})\|_{\infty}\cdot\|\hat{\hat{\rho}}^{\hat{M}}_{l_{x}xl_{y}y}\|_{1}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 4\epsilon^{1/8}L^{-1/2}\sum_{x,y}p^{XY}(x,y)\frac{p^{X}(x)}{q^{X}(x)}\|\rho(X)^{M}_{xy}\|_{1}

\displaystyle~~~{}+4f(e)L^{-2}\sum_{l_{x},x,l_{y},y}p^{XY}(x,y)\cdot\frac{p^{X}(x)}{q^{X}(x)}\|\Pi^{M}_{x}(1)\circ\rho^{M}_{x})\|_{\infty}\cdot\|\hat{\hat{\rho}}^{\hat{M}}_{l_{x}xl_{y}y}\|_{1}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 4\epsilon^{1/8}L^{-1/2}\sum_{x,y}p^{XY}(x,y)\frac{p^{X}(x)}{q^{X}(x)}\|\rho(X)^{M}_{xy}\|_{1}

\displaystyle~~~{}+4f(e)L^{-2}\sum_{l_{x},x,l_{y},y}p^{XY}(x,y)\cdot\frac{(1+4\sqrt{e}+2\sqrt{\epsilon})2^{D^{\epsilon}_{\infty}(\rho^{XM}\|q^{X}\otimes\rho^{M})}}{|F^{\prime}_{\rho}|}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 4\epsilon^{1/8}L^{-1/2}\sum_{x,y}p^{XY}(x,y)\frac{p^{X}(x)}{q^{X}(x)}\|\rho(X)^{M}_{xy}\|_{1}+\frac{4(1+4\sqrt{e}+2\sqrt{\epsilon})f(e)2^{D^{\epsilon}_{\infty}(\rho^{XM}\|q^{X}\otimes\rho^{M})}}{|F^{\prime}_{\rho}|}.

The proof of the lemma is now complete.

\square

Lemma 16

\displaystyle L^{-3}\sum_{l_{x},x,l_{y},y,l^{\prime}_{x},x^{\prime}}q^{Y}(y)\bar{q}^{XX^{\prime}}(x,x^{\prime})\Tr\left[\left(\frac{p^{XY}(x^{\prime},y)}{q^{X}(x^{\prime})q^{Y}(y)}\hat{\rho}^{\hat{M}}_{l^{\prime}_{x}x^{\prime}l_{y}y}\right)\left(\frac{p^{XY}(x,y)}{q^{X}(x)q^{Y}(y)}\hat{\hat{\rho}}^{\hat{M}}_{l_{x}xl_{y}y}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 4\epsilon^{1/8}L^{-1/2}\sum_{x,y}p^{XY}(x,y)\frac{p^{Y}(y)}{q^{Y}(y)}\|\rho(Y)^{M}_{xy}\|_{1}+\frac{4(1+4\sqrt{e}+2\sqrt{\epsilon})f(e)2^{D^{\epsilon}_{\infty}(\rho^{YM}\|q^{Y}\otimes\rho^{M})}}{|F^{\prime}_{\rho}|}.

Proof:

Similar to proof of Lemma
15
above.

\square

Lemma 17

\displaystyle L^{-4}\sum_{l_{x},x,l_{y},y,l^{\prime}_{x},x^{\prime},l^{\prime}_{y},y^{\prime}}\bar{q}^{XX^{\prime}}(x,x^{\prime})\bar{q}^{YY^{\prime}}(y,y^{\prime})\Tr\left[\left(\frac{p^{XY}(x^{\prime},y^{\prime})}{q^{X}(x^{\prime})q^{Y}(y^{\prime})}\hat{\rho}^{\hat{M}}_{l^{\prime}_{x}x^{\prime}l^{\prime}_{y}y^{\prime}}\right)\left(\frac{p^{XY}(x,y)}{q^{X}(x)q^{Y}(y)}\hat{\hat{\rho}}^{\hat{M}}_{l_{x}xl_{y}y}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 8\epsilon^{1/8}L^{-1/2}\sum_{x,y}p^{XY}(x,y)\|\rho()^{M}_{xy}\|_{1}+\frac{(1+g(e)+2\sqrt{\epsilon})}{|F^{\prime}_{\rho}|}.~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~

Proof:
