&&~~~~~~~~~~~~~~(T_{\epsilon^{1/4}}^{A^{\prime}_{1}\rightarrow\hat{A}^{\prime}_{1}}\otimes T_{\epsilon^{1/4}}^{A^{\prime}_{2}\rightarrow\hat{A}^{\prime}_{2}}\otimes\mathbb{I}^{E})(\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{A^{\prime}_{1}A^{\prime}_{2}E}-\Pi(3)^{A^{\prime}_{1}A^{\prime}_{2}E})\},\\
\hat{\hat{\rho}}^{\hat{A}_{1}\hat{A}_{2}R}&:=&\hat{\Pi}^{\hat{A}_{1}\hat{A}_{2}R}\circ\rho^{\hat{A}_{1}\hat{A}_{2}R},~~\hat{\hat{\tau}}^{\hat{A}^{\prime}_{1}\hat{A}^{\prime}_{2}E}:=\hat{\Pi}^{\hat{A}^{\prime}_{1}\hat{A}^{\prime}_{2}E}\circ\tau^{\hat{A}^{\prime}_{1}\hat{A}^{\prime}_{2}E},\\
\sigma(U^{\hat{A}_{1}},U^{\hat{A}_{2}})^{ER}&:=&(\mathcal{T}^{\hat{A}_{1}\hat{A}_{2}\rightarrow E}\otimes\mathbb{I}^{R})((U^{\hat{A}_{1}}\otimes U^{\hat{A}_{2}}\otimes\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{R})\circ\rho^{\hat{A}_{1}\hat{A}_{2}R}),\\
\hat{\sigma}(U^{\hat{A}_{1}},U^{\hat{A}_{2}})^{ER}&:=&(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{\hat{A}_{1}\hat{A}_{2}\rightarrow E}\otimes\mathbb{I}^{R})((U^{\hat{A}_{1}}\otimes U^{\hat{A}_{2}}\otimes\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{R})\circ\hat{\rho}^{\hat{A}_{1}\hat{A}_{2}R}),\\
\hat{\hat{\sigma}}(U^{\hat{A}_{1}},U^{\hat{A}_{2}})^{ER}&:=&(\hat{\hat{\mathcal{T}}}^{\hat{A}_{1}\hat{A}_{2}\rightarrow E}\otimes\mathbb{I}^{R})((U^{\hat{A}_{1}}\otimes U^{\hat{A}_{2}}\otimes\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{R})\circ\hat{\hat{\rho}}^{\hat{A}_{1}\hat{A}_{2}R}),\\
\end{array}

(77)

where
\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{\hat{A}_{1}\hat{A}_{2}\rightarrow E}
,

\hat{\hat{\mathcal{T}}}^{\hat{A}_{1}\hat{A}_{2}\rightarrow E}
are the completely positive
superoperators arising as the inverse Choi images of

\hat{\tau}^{\hat{A}^{\prime}_{1}\hat{A}^{\prime}_{2}E}
,
\hat{\hat{\tau}}^{\hat{A}^{\prime}_{1}\hat{A}^{\prime}_{2}E}
.
Note that
\rho^{A_{1}A_{2}R}
,
\rho^{\hat{A}_{1}\hat{A}_{2}R}
,

\hat{\rho}^{\hat{A}_{1}\hat{A}_{2}R}
are normalised density matrices, and

\hat{\hat{\rho}}^{\hat{A}_{1}\hat{A}_{2}R}
,
\tau^{A^{\prime}_{1}A^{\prime}_{2}E}
,
\tau^{\hat{A}^{\prime}_{1}\hat{A}^{\prime}_{2}E}
,

\hat{\tau}^{\hat{A}^{\prime}_{1}\hat{A}^{\prime}_{2}E}
,
\hat{\hat{\tau}}^{\hat{A}^{\prime}_{1}\hat{A}^{\prime}_{2}E}
are subnormalised
density matrices. Even though
\hat{\tau}^{\hat{A}^{\prime}_{1}\hat{A}^{\prime}_{2}E}
,

\hat{\hat{\tau}}^{\hat{A}^{\prime}_{1}\hat{A}^{\prime}_{2}E}
are subnormalised, their inverse Choi images

\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{\hat{A}_{1}\hat{A}_{2}\rightarrow E}
,
\hat{\hat{\mathcal{T}}}^{\hat{A}_{1}\hat{A}_{2}\rightarrow E}

can increase trace but by at most a multiplicative factor of
4
.

The following properties can be proved easily as in
Sections
4
,
7
.

\begin{array}[]{rcl}\frac{(1-\sqrt{\epsilon})(\Tr\tau)}{|F^{\prime}_{\tau}||F^{\prime}_{\rho}|}\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{F^{\prime}_{\tau}F^{\prime}_{\rho}}&\leq&\tau^{E}\otimes\rho^{R}\leq\frac{(1+\sqrt{\epsilon})(\Tr\tau)}{|F^{\prime}_{\tau}||F^{\prime}_{\rho}|}\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{F^{\prime}_{\tau}F^{\prime}_{\rho}},\\
\hat{\rho}^{\hat{A}_{1}R}&=&(T_{\epsilon^{1/4}}^{A_{1}\rightarrow\hat{A}_{1}}\otimes\mathbb{I}^{R})(\rho^{A_{1}R}),~\hat{\rho}^{\hat{A}_{2}R}=(T_{\epsilon^{1/4}}^{A_{2}\rightarrow\hat{A}_{2}}\otimes\mathbb{I}^{R})(\rho^{A_{2}R}),\\
\hat{\tau}^{\hat{A}^{\prime}_{1}R}&=&(T_{\epsilon^{1/4}}^{A^{\prime}_{1}\rightarrow\hat{A}^{\prime}_{1}}\otimes\mathbb{I}^{R})(\tau^{A^{\prime}_{1}R}),~\hat{\tau}^{\hat{A}^{\prime}_{2}R}=(T_{\epsilon^{1/4}}^{A^{\prime}_{2}\rightarrow\hat{A}^{\prime}_{2}}\otimes\mathbb{I}^{R})(\tau^{A^{\prime}_{2}R}),\\
\|\hat{\rho}^{\hat{A}_{1}\hat{A}_{2}R}-\rho^{\hat{A}_{1}\hat{A}_{2}R}\|_{1}&\leq&4\sqrt{2}\epsilon^{1/8},~~\|\hat{\tau}^{\hat{A}^{\prime}_{1}\hat{A}^{\prime}_{2}R}-\tau^{\hat{A}^{\prime}_{1}\hat{A}^{\prime}_{2}R}\|_{1}\leq 4\sqrt{2}\epsilon^{1/8}(\Tr\tau),\\
\Tr[\hat{\hat{\rho}}^{\hat{A}_{1}\hat{A}_{2}R}]&\geq&1-121\epsilon^{1/4},~~\Tr[\hat{\hat{\tau}}^{\hat{A}^{\prime}_{1}\hat{A}^{\prime}_{2}E}]\geq(1-121\epsilon^{1/4})(\Tr\tau),\\
\implies\|\hat{\hat{\rho}}^{\hat{A}_{1}\hat{A}_{2}R}-\rho^{\hat{A}_{1}\hat{A}_{2}R}\|_{1}&\leq&22\epsilon^{1/8},~~\|\hat{\hat{\rho}}^{\hat{A}_{1}\hat{A}_{2}R}-\hat{\rho}^{\hat{A}_{1}\hat{A}_{2}R}\|_{1}\leq 28\epsilon^{1/8},\\
\implies\|\hat{\hat{\tau}}^{\hat{A}^{\prime}_{1}\hat{A}^{\prime}_{2}E}-\tau^{\hat{A}^{\prime}_{1}\hat{A}^{\prime}_{2}E}\|_{1}&\leq&22\epsilon^{1/8}(\Tr\tau),~~\|\hat{\hat{\tau}}^{\hat{A}^{\prime}_{1}\hat{A}^{\prime}_{2}E}-\hat{\tau}^{\hat{A}^{\prime}_{1}\hat{A}^{\prime}_{2}E}\|_{1}\leq 28\epsilon^{1/8}(\Tr\tau).\end{array}

(78)

Following an argument in the proof of Proposition 3 in

[
Sen24
]
, which in turn originated from a method in

[
SDTR13
]
, and using Equation
78
and the
fact that
\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{\hat{A}_{1}\hat{A}_{2}\rightarrow E}
can increase the trace
by at most a factor of
4
, we get

\begin{array}[]{rcl}\lx@intercol\lx@add@cssclass{ltx_eqn_lefteqn}$\displaystyle\E_{U^{\hat{A}_{1}},U^{\hat{A}_{2}}}[\|\sigma(U^{\hat{A}_{1}},U^{\hat{A}_{2}})^{ER}-\hat{\sigma}(U^{\hat{A}_{1}},U^{\hat{A}_{2}})^{ER}\|_{1}]$\mbox{}\hfil\lx@intercol\\
&\leq&\E_{U^{\hat{A}_{1}},U^{\hat{A}_{2}}}[\|(\mathcal{T}^{\hat{A}_{1}\hat{A}_{2}\rightarrow E}\otimes\mathbb{I}^{R})((U^{\hat{A}_{1}}\otimes U^{\hat{A}_{2}}\otimes\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{R})\circ\rho^{\hat{A}_{1}\hat{A}_{2}R})\\
&&~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~{}-(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{\hat{A}_{1}\hat{A}_{2}\rightarrow E}\otimes\mathbb{I}^{R})((U^{\hat{A}_{1}}\otimes U^{\hat{A}_{2}}\otimes\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{R})\circ\hat{\rho}^{\hat{A}_{1}\hat{A}_{2}R})\|_{1}]\\
&\leq&\E_{U^{\hat{A}_{1}},U^{\hat{A}_{2}}}[\|(\mathcal{T}^{\hat{A}_{1}\hat{A}_{2}\rightarrow E}\otimes\mathbb{I}^{R})((U^{\hat{A}_{1}}\otimes U^{\hat{A}_{2}}\otimes\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{R})\circ\rho^{\hat{A}_{1}\hat{A}_{2}R})\\
&&~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~{}-(\mathcal{T}^{\hat{A}_{1}\hat{A}_{2}\rightarrow E}\otimes\mathbb{I}^{R})((U^{\hat{A}_{1}}\otimes U^{\hat{A}_{2}}\otimes\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{R})\circ\hat{\rho}^{\hat{A}_{1}\hat{A}_{2}R})\|_{1}]\\
&&{}+\E_{U^{\hat{A}_{1}},U^{\hat{A}_{2}}}[\|(\mathcal{T}^{\hat{A}_{1}\hat{A}_{2}\rightarrow E}\otimes\mathbb{I}^{R})((U^{\hat{A}_{1}}\otimes U^{\hat{A}_{2}}\otimes\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{R})\circ\hat{\rho}^{\hat{A}_{1}\hat{A}_{2}R})\\
&&~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~{}-(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{\hat{A}_{1}\hat{A}_{2}\rightarrow E}\otimes\mathbb{I}^{R})((U^{\hat{A}_{1}}\otimes U^{\hat{A}_{2}}\otimes\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{R})\circ\hat{\rho}^{\hat{A}_{1}\hat{A}_{2}R})\|_{1}]\\
&\leq&4\|\rho^{\hat{A}_{1}\hat{A}_{2}R}-\hat{\rho}^{\hat{A}_{1}\hat{A}_{2}R}\|_{1}+\|\tau^{\hat{A}^{\prime}_{1}\hat{A}^{\prime}_{2}E}-\hat{\tau}^{\hat{A}^{\prime}_{1}\hat{A}^{\prime}_{2}E}\|_{1}\leq 20\sqrt{2}\epsilon^{1/8},\\
\mbox{Similarly,}~~~~\hbox to0.0pt{$\displaystyle\E_{U^{\hat{A}_{1}},U^{\hat{A}_{2}}}[\|\hat{\hat{\sigma}}(U^{\hat{A}_{1}},U^{\hat{A}_{2}})^{ER}-\hat{\sigma}(U^{\hat{A}_{1}},U^{\hat{A}_{2}})^{ER}\|_{1}]$\hss}\\
&\leq&4\|\hat{\hat{\rho}}^{\hat{A}_{1}\hat{A}_{2}R}-\hat{\rho}^{\hat{A}_{1}\hat{A}_{2}R}\|_{1}+\|\hat{\hat{\tau}}^{\hat{A}^{\prime}_{1}\hat{A}^{\prime}_{2}E}-\hat{\tau}^{\hat{A}^{\prime}_{1}\hat{A}^{\prime}_{2}E}\|_{1}\leq 140\epsilon^{1/8}.\end{array}

(79)

Define the set

\mathsf{Good}:=\{(U^{\hat{A}_{1}},U^{\hat{A}_{2}}):\|\hat{\hat{\sigma}}(U^{\hat{A}_{1}},U^{\hat{A}_{2}})^{ER}-\hat{\sigma}(U^{\hat{A}_{1}},U^{\hat{A}_{2}})^{ER}]\|_{1}<140\epsilon^{1/16}\}.

Applying Markov’s inequality to the last inequality in
Equation
79
, we get

\Pr_{U^{\hat{A}_{1}},U^{\hat{A}_{2}}}[\mathsf{Good}]>1-\epsilon^{1/16}.

Applying Lemma
1
, we get

\begin{array}[]{c}\forall(U^{\hat{A}_{1}},U^{\hat{A}_{2}})\in\mathsf{Good}:\exists(\sigma^{\prime}(U^{\hat{A}_{1}},U^{\hat{A}_{2}}))^{ER}:\\
\|(\sigma^{\prime}(U^{\hat{A}_{1}},U^{\hat{A}_{2}}))^{ER}-\hat{\sigma}(U^{\hat{A}_{1}},U^{\hat{A}_{2}})^{ER}\|_{1}<12\epsilon^{1/32},\\
(\sigma^{\prime}(U^{\hat{A}_{1}},U^{\hat{A}_{2}}))^{ER}\leq\hat{\sigma}(U^{\hat{A}_{1}},U^{\hat{A}_{2}})^{ER},\\
\|(\sigma^{\prime}(U^{\hat{A}_{1}},U^{\hat{A}_{2}}))^{ER}\|_{2}^{2}\leq(1+24\epsilon^{1/32})\Tr[\hat{\sigma}(U^{\hat{A}_{1}},U^{\hat{A}_{2}})^{ER}\hat{\hat{\sigma}}(U^{\hat{A}_{1}},U^{\hat{A}_{2}})^{ER}],\\
\forall(U^{\hat{A}_{1}},U^{\hat{A}_{2}})\not\in\mathsf{Good}:(\sigma^{\prime}(U^{\hat{A}_{1}},U^{\hat{A}_{2}}))^{ER}:=\hbox{\small l\kern-3.5pt\normalsize 0}^{ER},\\
\implies\forall(U^{\hat{A}_{1}},U^{\hat{A}_{2}}):(\sigma^{\prime}(U^{\hat{A}_{1}},U^{\hat{A}_{2}}))^{ER}\leq\hat{\sigma}(U^{\hat{A}_{1}},U^{\hat{A}_{2}})^{ER},\\
\|(\sigma^{\prime}((U^{\hat{A}_{1}},U^{\hat{A}_{2}})))^{ER}\|_{2}^{2}\leq(1+24\epsilon^{1/32})\Tr[\hat{\sigma}((U^{\hat{A}_{1}},U^{\hat{A}_{2}}))^{ER}\hat{\hat{\sigma}}((U^{\hat{A}_{1}},U^{\hat{A}_{2}}))^{ER}].\end{array}

(80)

Using Equations
79
and
80
and the
property that
\mathcal{T}^{\hat{A}_{1}\hat{A}_{2}\rightarrow E}
increases the trace
by at most a factor of
4
, we get

\displaystyle\E_{U^{\hat{A}_{1}},U^{\hat{A}_{2}}}[\|\sigma(U^{\hat{A}_{1}},U^{\hat{A}_{2}})^{ER}-(\sigma^{\prime}(U^{\hat{A}_{1}},U^{\hat{A}_{2}}))^{ER}\|_{1}]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\E_{(U^{\hat{A}_{1}},U^{\hat{A}_{2}})\in\mathsf{Good}}[\|\sigma(U^{\hat{A}_{1}},U^{\hat{A}_{2}})^{ER}-(\sigma^{\prime}(U^{\hat{A}_{1}},U^{\hat{A}_{2}}))^{ER}\|_{1}]

\displaystyle{}+\E_{(U^{\hat{A}_{1}},U^{\hat{A}_{2}})\not\in\mathsf{Good}}[\|\sigma(U^{\hat{A}_{1}},U^{\hat{A}_{2}})^{ER}\|_{1}]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\E_{(U^{\hat{A}_{1}},U^{\hat{A}_{2}})\in\mathsf{Good}}[\|\sigma(U^{\hat{A}_{1}},U^{\hat{A}_{2}})^{ER}-\hat{\sigma}(U^{\hat{A}_{1}},U^{\hat{A}_{2}})^{ER}\|_{1}]

\displaystyle{}+\E_{(U^{\hat{A}_{1}},U^{\hat{A}_{2}})\in\mathsf{Good}}[\|\hat{\sigma}(U^{\hat{A}_{1}},U^{\hat{A}_{2}})^{ER}-(\sigma^{\prime}(U^{\hat{A}_{1}},U^{\hat{A}_{2}}))^{ER}\|_{1}]+4(1-\Pr_{U^{\hat{A}_{1}},U^{\hat{A}_{2}}}[\mathsf{Good}])

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\E_{U^{\hat{A}_{1}},U^{\hat{A}_{2}}}[\|\sigma(U^{\hat{A}_{1}},U^{\hat{A}_{2}})^{ER}-\hat{\sigma}(U^{\hat{A}_{1}},U^{\hat{A}_{2}})^{ER}\|_{1}]+12\epsilon^{1/32}+4\epsilon^{1/16}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 20\sqrt{2}\epsilon^{1/8}+12\epsilon^{1/32}+4\epsilon^{1/16}<45\epsilon^{1/32}.

Thus in order to prove the present proposition, from
Equation
75
, it suffices to show that

\E_{U^{\hat{A}_{1}},U^{\hat{A}_{2}}}[\|(\sigma^{\prime}(U^{\hat{A}_{1}},U^{\hat{A}_{2}}))^{ER}-\tau^{E}\otimes\rho^{R}\|_{1}]<36\epsilon^{1/64}.

(81)

Define

\Pi^{ER}:=\mathrm{supp}(\tau^{E}\otimes\rho^{R})=F^{\prime}_{\tau}\otimes F^{\prime}_{\rho}
.
Observe that for all
(U^{\hat{A}_{1}},U^{\hat{A}_{2}})
,

\mathrm{supp}((\sigma^{\prime}(U^{\hat{A}_{1}},U^{\hat{A}_{2}}))^{ER})\leq\mathrm{supp}(\hat{\sigma}(U^{\hat{A}_{1}},U^{\hat{A}_{2}})^{ER})\leq\Pi^{ER},~\mathrm{supp}(\sigma(U^{\hat{A}_{1}},U^{\hat{A}_{2}})^{ER})\leq\Pi^{ER},

where the first operator inequality follows from
Equation
80
and the second operator inequality
follows from the fact that
\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{\hat{A}_{1}\hat{A}_{2}\rightarrow E}
is
the completely positive superoperator that projects from

\hat{A}_{1}\otimes\hat{A}_{2}
onto the subspace

T_{\epsilon^{1/4}}^{A_{1}\rightarrow\hat{A}_{1}}\otimes T_{\epsilon^{1/4}}^{A_{2}\rightarrow\hat{A}_{2}},

followed by rotating the subspace onto

(A_{1}\otimes|0\rangle\langle 0|^{\mathbb{C}^{2}})\otimes(A_{2}\otimes|0\rangle\langle 0|^{\mathbb{C}^{2}})
,
followed by applying
4\mathcal{T}^{A_{1}A_{2}\rightarrow E}
.
By Proposition
1
,
Equation
81
and the convexity of the square function,
it suffices to show

\begin{array}[]{c}\sqrt{\Tr\Pi^{ER}}\cdot\E_{U^{\hat{A}_{1}},U^{\hat{A}_{2}}}[\|(\sigma^{\prime}(U^{\hat{A}_{1}},U^{\hat{A}_{2}}))^{ER}-\tau^{E}\otimes\rho^{R}\|_{1}]<36\epsilon^{1/64},\\
\implies~\mbox{it suffices to show}~~\E_{U^{\hat{A}_{1}},U^{\hat{A}_{2}}}[\|(\sigma^{\prime}(U^{\hat{A}_{1}},U^{\hat{A}_{2}}))^{ER}-\tau^{E}\otimes\rho^{R}\|_{2}^{2}]<\frac{1296\epsilon^{1/32}}{F^{\prime}_{\tau}F^{\prime}_{\rho}}.\end{array}

(82)

Using Equations
78
,
80
, the
method of the proof of Proposition 3 of
[
Sen24
]
and the
fact that
\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{\hat{A}_{1}\hat{A}_{2}\rightarrow E}
is a completely positive
superoperator that increases the trace by at most a factor of
4
, we have,

\displaystyle\E_{U^{\hat{A}_{1}},U^{\hat{A}_{2}}}[\|(\sigma^{\prime}(U^{\hat{A}_{1}},U^{\hat{A}_{2}}))^{ER}-\tau^{E}\otimes\rho^{R}\|_{2}^{2}]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\E_{U^{\hat{A}_{1}},U^{\hat{A}_{2}}}[\|(\sigma^{\prime}(U^{\hat{A}_{1}},U^{\hat{A}_{2}}))^{ER}\|_{2}^{2}]+\|\tau^{E}\otimes\rho^{R}\|_{2}^{2}-2\E_{U^{\hat{A}_{1}},U^{\hat{A}_{2}}}[\Tr[(\sigma^{\prime}(U^{\hat{A}_{1}},U^{\hat{A}_{2}}))^{ER}(\tau^{E}\otimes\rho^{R})]]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\E_{U^{\hat{A}_{1}},U^{\hat{A}_{2}}}[\|(\sigma^{\prime}(U^{\hat{A}_{1}},U^{\hat{A}_{2}}))^{ER}\|_{2}^{2}]+\frac{(1+\sqrt{\epsilon})^{2}(\Tr\tau)^{2}}{|F^{\prime}_{\tau}|^{2}|F^{\prime}_{\rho}|^{2}}\Tr[\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{F^{\prime}_{\tau}F^{\prime}_{\rho}}]

\displaystyle{}-\frac{2(1-\sqrt{\epsilon})(\Tr\tau)}{|F^{\prime}_{\tau}||F^{\prime}_{\rho}|}\E_{U^{\hat{A}_{1}},U^{\hat{A}_{2}}}[\Tr[(\sigma^{\prime}(U^{\hat{A}_{1}},U^{\hat{A}_{2}}))^{ER}\Pi^{ER}]]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\E_{U^{\hat{A}_{1}},U^{\hat{A}_{2}}}[\|(\sigma^{\prime}(U^{\hat{A}_{1}},U^{\hat{A}_{2}}))^{ER}\|_{2}^{2}]+\frac{(1+\sqrt{\epsilon})^{2}(\Tr\tau)^{2}}{|F^{\prime}_{\tau}||F^{\prime}_{\rho}|}

\displaystyle{}-\frac{2(1-\sqrt{\epsilon})(\Tr\tau)}{|F^{\prime}_{\tau}||F^{\prime}_{\rho}|}\E_{(U^{\hat{A}_{1}},U^{\hat{A}_{2}})\in\mathsf{Good}}[\Tr[(\sigma^{\prime}(U^{\hat{A}_{1}},U^{\hat{A}_{2}}))^{ER}\Pi^{ER}]]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\E_{U^{\hat{A}_{1}},U^{\hat{A}_{2}}}[\|(\sigma^{\prime}(U^{\hat{A}_{1}},U^{\hat{A}_{2}}))^{ER}\|_{2}^{2}]+\frac{(1+\sqrt{\epsilon})^{2}(\Tr\tau)^{2}}{|F^{\prime}_{\tau}||F^{\prime}_{\rho}|}

\displaystyle{}-\frac{2(1-\sqrt{\epsilon})(\Tr\tau)}{|F^{\prime}_{\tau}||F^{\prime}_{\rho}|}\E_{(U^{\hat{A}_{1}},U^{\hat{A}_{2}})\in\mathsf{Good}}[\Tr[\hat{\sigma}(U^{\hat{A}_{1}},U^{\hat{A}_{2}})^{ER}\Pi^{ER}]]

\displaystyle{}+\frac{2(1-\sqrt{\epsilon})(\Tr\tau)}{|F^{\prime}_{\tau}||F^{\prime}_{\rho}|}\E_{(U^{\hat{A}_{1}},U^{\hat{A}_{2}})\in\mathsf{Good}}[\|\hat{\sigma}(U^{\hat{A}_{1}},U^{\hat{A}_{2}})^{ER}-(\sigma^{\prime}(U^{\hat{A}_{1}},U^{\hat{A}_{2}}))^{ER}\|_{1}]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\E_{U^{\hat{A}_{1}},U^{\hat{A}_{2}}}[\|(\sigma^{\prime}(U^{\hat{A}_{1}},U^{\hat{A}_{2}}))^{ER}\|_{2}^{2}]+\frac{(1+\sqrt{\epsilon})^{2}(\Tr\tau)^{2}}{|F^{\prime}_{\tau}||F^{\prime}_{\rho}|}

\displaystyle{}-\frac{2(1-\sqrt{\epsilon})(\Tr\tau)}{|F^{\prime}_{\tau}||F^{\prime}_{\rho}|}\E_{U^{\hat{A}_{1}},U^{\hat{A}_{2}}}[\Tr[\hat{\sigma}(U^{\hat{A}_{1}},U^{\hat{A}_{2}})^{ER}\Pi^{ER}]]

\displaystyle{}+\frac{8(1-\sqrt{\epsilon})(\Tr\tau)(1-\Pr_{U^{\hat{A}_{1}},U^{\hat{A}_{2}}}[\mathsf{Good}])}{|F^{\prime}_{\tau}||F^{\prime}_{\rho}|}+\frac{24\epsilon^{1/32}(1-\sqrt{\epsilon})(\Tr\tau)}{|F^{\prime}_{\tau}||F^{\prime}_{\rho}|}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\E_{U^{\hat{A}_{1}},U^{\hat{A}_{2}}}[\|(\sigma^{\prime}(U^{\hat{A}_{1}},U^{\hat{A}_{2}}))^{ER}\|_{2}^{2}]+\frac{(1+\sqrt{\epsilon})^{2}(\Tr\tau)^{2}}{|F^{\prime}_{\tau}||F^{\prime}_{\rho}|}+\frac{32\epsilon^{1/32}}{|F^{\prime}_{\tau}||F^{\prime}_{\rho}|}

\displaystyle{}-\frac{2(1-\sqrt{\epsilon})(\Tr\tau)}{|F^{\prime}_{\tau}||F^{\prime}_{\rho}|}\Tr[(\tau^{E}\otimes\tau^{R})\Pi^{ER}]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{32\epsilon^{1/32}}{|F^{\prime}_{\tau}||F^{\prime}_{\rho}|}+\E_{U^{\hat{A}_{1}},U^{\hat{A}_{2}}}[\|(\sigma^{\prime}(U^{\hat{A}_{1}},U^{\hat{A}_{2}}))^{ER}\|_{2}^{2}]+\frac{(1+\sqrt{\epsilon})^{2}(\Tr\tau)^{2}}{|F^{\prime}_{\tau}||F^{\prime}_{\rho}|}-\frac{2(1-\sqrt{\epsilon})(\Tr\tau)^{2}}{|F^{\prime}_{\tau}||F^{\prime}_{\rho}|}

\displaystyle<

\displaystyle\frac{32\epsilon^{1/32}}{|F^{\prime}_{\tau}||F^{\prime}_{\rho}|}+\E_{U^{\hat{A}_{1}},U^{\hat{A}_{2}}}[\|(\sigma^{\prime}(U^{\hat{A}_{1}},U^{\hat{A}_{2}}))^{ER}\|_{2}^{2}]-\frac{(1-5\sqrt{\epsilon})(\Tr\tau)^{2}}{|F^{\prime}_{\tau}||F^{\prime}_{\rho}|}.

Thus from Equations
82
,
80
, it
suffices to show that

\begin{array}[]{c}\E_{U^{\hat{A}_{1}},U^{\hat{A}_{2}}}[\|(\sigma^{\prime}(U^{\hat{A}_{1}},U^{\hat{A}_{2}}))^{ER}\|_{2}^{2}]\leq\frac{(1+1259\epsilon^{1/32})(\Tr\tau)^{2}}{|F^{\prime}_{\tau}||F^{\prime}_{\rho}|},\\
\implies~\mbox{suffices to show }~~\E_{U^{\hat{A}_{1}},U^{\hat{A}_{2}}}[\Tr[\hat{\sigma}(U^{\hat{A}_{1}},U^{\hat{A}_{2}})^{ER}\hat{\hat{\sigma}}(U^{\hat{A}_{1}},U^{\hat{A}_{2}})^{ER}]]\leq\frac{(1+30\epsilon^{1/32})(\Tr\tau)^{2}}{|F^{\prime}_{\tau}||F^{\prime}_{\rho}|}.\end{array}

(83)

Following the arguments in
[
CNS21
]
involving
the use of the
swap trick
and the
twirling operator
over
the Haar measure, we get

\begin{array}[]{rcl}\lx@intercol\lx@add@cssclass{ltx_eqn_lefteqn}$\displaystyle\E_{U^{\hat{A}_{1}},U^{\hat{A}_{2}}}[\Tr[\hat{\sigma}(U^{\hat{A}_{1}},U^{\hat{A}_{2}})^{ER}\hat{\hat{\sigma}}(U^{\hat{A}_{1}},U^{\hat{A}_{2}})^{ER}]]$\mbox{}\hfil\lx@intercol\\
&=&\alpha_{0}\Tr[\hat{\rho}^{R}\hat{\hat{\rho}}^{R}]+\alpha_{1}\Tr[\hat{\rho}^{\hat{A}_{1}R}\hat{\hat{\rho}}^{\hat{A}_{1}R}]+\alpha_{2}\Tr[\hat{\rho}^{\hat{A}_{2}R}\hat{\hat{\rho}}^{\hat{A}_{2}R}]+\alpha_{12}\Tr[\hat{\rho}^{\hat{A}_{1}\hat{A}_{2}R}\hat{\hat{\rho}}^{\hat{A}_{1}\hat{A}_{2}R}],\end{array}

(84)

where the 4-tuple
(\alpha_{0},\alpha_{1},\alpha_{2},\alpha_{12})
is
defined to be the solution of the following linear equation:

\begin{array}[]{rcl}\left[\begin{array}[]{c}\alpha_{0}\\
\alpha_{1}\\
\alpha_{2}\\
\alpha_{12}\end{array}\right]&=&\frac{|A_{1}||A_{2}|}{(|A_{1}|^{2}-1)(|A_{2}|^{2}-1)}\cdot\\
&&~~~\left[\begin{array}[]{c c c c}|A_{1}||A_{2}|&-|A_{2}|&-|A_{1}|&1\\
-|A_{2}|&|A_{1}||A_{2}|&1&-|A_{1}|\\
-|A_{1}|&1&|A_{1}||A_{2}|&-|A_{2}|\\
1&-|A_{1}|&-|A_{2}|&|A_{1}||A_{2}|\end{array}\right]\left[\begin{array}[]{c}\Tr[\hat{\tau}^{E}\hat{\hat{\tau}}^{E}]\\
\Tr[\hat{\tau}^{\hat{A}^{\prime}_{1}E}\hat{\hat{\tau}}^{\hat{A}^{\prime}_{1}E}]\\
\Tr[\hat{\tau}^{\hat{A}^{\prime}_{2}E}\hat{\hat{\tau}}^{\hat{A}^{\prime}_{2}E}]\\
\Tr[\hat{\tau}^{\hat{A}^{\prime}_{1}\hat{A}^{\prime}_{2}E}\hat{\hat{\tau}}^{\hat{A}^{\prime}_{1}\hat{A}^{\prime}_{2}E}]\end{array}\right].\end{array}

We bound the terms
\alpha_{0}
,
\alpha_{1}
,
\alpha_{2}
,
\alpha_{12}

as follows, using Fact
3
.

\begin{array}[]{rcl}\alpha_{0}&=&\frac{|A_{1}||A_{2}|}{(|A_{1}|^{2}-1)(|A_{2}|^{2}-1)}\cdot(|A_{1}||A_{2}|\cdot\Tr[\hat{\tau}^{E}\hat{\hat{\tau}}^{E}]-|A_{2}|\cdot\Tr[\hat{\tau}^{\hat{A}^{\prime}_{1}E}\hat{\hat{\tau}}^{\hat{A}^{\prime}_{1}E}]\\
&&~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~{}-|A_{1}|\cdot\Tr[\hat{\tau}^{\hat{A}^{\prime}_{2}E}\hat{\hat{\tau}}^{\hat{A}^{\prime}_{2}E}]+\Tr[\hat{\tau}^{\hat{A}^{\prime}_{1}\hat{A}^{\prime}_{2}E}\hat{\hat{\tau}}^{\hat{A}^{\prime}_{1}\hat{A}^{\prime}_{2}E}])\\
&\leq&\frac{|A_{1}||A_{2}|}{(|A_{1}|^{2}-1)(|A_{2}|^{2}-1)}\cdot(|A_{1}||A_{2}|\cdot\Tr[\hat{\tau}^{E}\hat{\hat{\tau}}^{E}]\\
&&~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~{}-|A_{1}|\cdot\Tr[\hat{\tau}^{\hat{A}^{\prime}_{2}E}\hat{\hat{\tau}}^{\hat{A}^{\prime}_{2}E}]+|A_{1}|\Tr[(\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{\hat{A}^{\prime}_{1}}\otimes\hat{\tau}^{\hat{A}^{\prime}_{2}E})\hat{\hat{\tau}}^{\hat{A}^{\prime}_{1}\hat{A}^{\prime}_{2}E}])\\
&=&\frac{|A_{1}||A_{2}|}{(|A_{1}|^{2}-1)(|A_{2}|^{2}-1)}\cdot(|A_{1}||A_{2}|\cdot\Tr[\hat{\tau}^{E}\hat{\hat{\tau}}^{E}]\\
&&~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~{}-|A_{1}|\cdot\Tr[\hat{\tau}^{\hat{A}^{\prime}_{2}E}\hat{\hat{\tau}}^{\hat{A}^{\prime}_{2}E}]+|A_{1}|\Tr[\hat{\tau}^{\hat{A}^{\prime}_{2}E}\hat{\hat{\tau}}^{\hat{A}^{\prime}_{2}E}])\\
&=&\frac{|A_{1}|^{2}|A_{2}|^{2}}{(|A_{1}|^{2}-1)(|A_{2}|^{2}-1)}\cdot\Tr[\hat{\tau}^{E}\hat{\hat{\tau}}^{E}],\\
\alpha_{1}&=&\frac{|A_{1}||A_{2}|}{(|A_{1}|^{2}-1)(|A_{2}|^{2}-1)}\cdot(-|A_{2}|\cdot\Tr[\hat{\tau}^{E}\hat{\hat{\tau}}^{E}]+|A_{1}||A_{2}|\cdot\Tr[\hat{\tau}^{\hat{A}^{\prime}_{1}E}\hat{\hat{\tau}}^{\hat{A}^{\prime}_{1}E}]\\
&&~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~{}+\Tr[\hat{\tau}^{\hat{A}^{\prime}_{2}E}\hat{\hat{\tau}}^{\hat{A}^{\prime}_{2}E}]-|A_{1}|\cdot\Tr[\hat{\tau}^{\hat{A}^{\prime}_{1}\hat{A}^{\prime}_{2}E}\hat{\hat{\tau}}^{\hat{A}^{\prime}_{1}\hat{A}^{\prime}_{2}E}])\\
&\leq&\frac{|A_{1}||A_{2}|}{(|A_{1}|^{2}-1)(|A_{2}|^{2}-1)}\cdot(-|A_{2}|\cdot\Tr[\hat{\tau}^{E}\hat{\hat{\tau}}^{E}]+|A_{1}||A_{2}|\cdot\Tr[\hat{\tau}^{\hat{A}^{\prime}_{1}E}\hat{\hat{\tau}}^{\hat{A}^{\prime}_{1}E}]\\
&&~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~{}+|A_{2}|\cdot\Tr[(\hbox{\small 1\kern-3.8pt\normalsize 1}^{\hat{A}^{\prime}_{2}}\otimes\hat{\tau}^{E})\hat{\hat{\tau}}^{\hat{A}^{\prime}_{2}E}])\\
&=&\frac{|A_{1}||A_{2}|}{(|A_{1}|^{2}-1)(|A_{2}|^{2}-1)}\cdot(-|A_{2}|\cdot\Tr[\hat{\tau}^{E}\hat{\hat{\tau}}^{E}]+|A_{1}||A_{2}|\cdot\Tr[\hat{\tau}^{\hat{A}^{\prime}_{1}E}\hat{\hat{\tau}}^{\hat{A}^{\prime}_{1}E}]\\
&&~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~{}+|A_{2}|\cdot\Tr[\hat{\tau}^{E}\hat{\hat{\tau}}^{E}]+)\\
&=&\frac{|A_{1}|^{2}|A_{2}|^{2}}{(|A_{1}|^{2}-1)(|A_{2}|^{2}-1)}\cdot\Tr[\hat{\tau}^{\hat{A}^{\prime}_{1}E}\hat{\hat{\tau}}^{\hat{A}^{\prime}_{1}E}],\\
\alpha_{2}&\leq&\frac{|A_{1}|^{2}|A_{2}|^{2}}{(|A_{1}|^{2}-1)(|A_{2}|^{2}-1)}\cdot\Tr[\hat{\tau}^{\hat{A}^{\prime}_{2}E}\hat{\hat{\tau}}^{\hat{A}^{\prime}_{2}E}]~~\mbox{similarly},\\
\alpha_{12}&=&\frac{|A_{1}||A_{2}|}{(|A_{1}|^{2}-1)(|A_{2}|^{2}-1)}\cdot(\Tr[\hat{\tau}^{E}\hat{\hat{\tau}}^{E}]-|A_{1}|\cdot\Tr[\hat{\tau}^{\hat{A}^{\prime}_{1}E}\hat{\hat{\tau}}^{\hat{A}^{\prime}_{1}E}]\\
&&~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~{}-|A_{2}|\cdot\Tr[\hat{\tau}^{\hat{A}^{\prime}_{2}E}\hat{\hat{\tau}}^{\hat{A}^{\prime}_{2}E}]+|A_{1}||A_{2}|\cdot\Tr[\hat{\tau}^{\hat{A}^{\prime}_{1}\hat{A}^{\prime}_{2}E}\hat{\hat{\tau}}^{\hat{A}^{\prime}_{1}\hat{A}^{\prime}_{2}E}])\\
&\leq&\frac{|A_{1}||A_{2}|}{(|A_{1}|^{2}-1)(|A_{2}|^{2}-1)}\cdot(\Tr[\hat{\tau}^{E}\hat{\hat{\tau}}^{E}]+|A_{1}||A_{2}|\cdot\Tr[\hat{\tau}^{\hat{A}^{\prime}_{1}\hat{A}^{\prime}_{2}E}\hat{\hat{\tau}}^{\hat{A}^{\prime}_{1}\hat{A}^{\prime}_{2}E}]).\end{array}

(85)

From Equations
84
,
85
we get,

\begin{array}[]{rcl}\lx@intercol\lx@add@cssclass{ltx_eqn_lefteqn}$\displaystyle\E_{U^{\hat{A}_{1}},U^{\hat{A}_{2}}}[\Tr[\hat{\sigma}(U^{\hat{A}_{1}},U^{\hat{A}_{2}})^{ER}\hat{\hat{\sigma}}(U^{\hat{A}_{1}},U^{\hat{A}_{2}})^{ER}]]$\mbox{}\hfil\lx@intercol\\
&\leq&\frac{|A_{1}|^{2}|A_{2}|^{2}}{(|A_{1}|^{2}-1)(|A_{2}|^{2}-1)}\cdot\Tr[\hat{\tau}^{E}\hat{\hat{\tau}}^{E}]\cdot\Tr[\hat{\rho}^{R}\hat{\hat{\rho}}^{R}]\\
&&{}+\frac{|A_{1}|^{2}|A_{2}|^{2}}{(|A_{1}|^{2}-1)(|A_{2}|^{2}-1)}\cdot\Tr[\hat{\tau}^{\hat{A}^{\prime}_{2}E}\hat{\hat{\tau}}^{\hat{A}^{\prime}_{2}E}]\cdot\Tr[\hat{\rho}^{\hat{A}_{1}R}\hat{\hat{\rho}}^{\hat{A}_{1}R}]\\
&&{}+\frac{|A_{1}|^{2}|A_{2}|^{2}}{(|A_{1}|^{2}-1)(|A_{2}|^{2}-1)}\cdot\Tr[\hat{\tau}^{\hat{A}^{\prime}_{2}E}\hat{\hat{\tau}}^{\hat{A}^{\prime}_{2}E}]\cdot\Tr[\hat{\rho}^{\hat{A}_{2}R}\hat{\hat{\rho}}^{\hat{A}_{2}R}]\\
&&{}+\frac{|A_{1}||A_{2}|}{(|A_{1}|^{2}-1)(|A_{2}|^{2}-1)}\cdot(\Tr[\hat{\tau}^{E}\hat{\hat{\tau}}^{E}]+|A_{1}||A_{2}|\cdot\Tr[\hat{\tau}^{\hat{A}^{\prime}_{1}\hat{A}^{\prime}_{2}E}\hat{\hat{\tau}}^{\hat{A}^{\prime}_{1}\hat{A}^{\prime}_{2}E}])\cdot\Tr[\hat{\rho}^{\hat{A}_{1}\hat{A}_{2}R}\hat{\hat{\rho}}^{\hat{A}_{1}\hat{A}_{2}R}].\end{array}

(86)

As in Section
7
, we can prove the following
lemma.

Lemma 25

\displaystyle\Tr[\hat{\tau}^{E}\hat{\hat{\tau}}^{E}]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{(1+\sqrt{\epsilon})(\Tr\tau)^{2}}{|F^{\prime}_{\tau}|},~~\Tr[\hat{\rho}^{R}\hat{\hat{\rho}}^{R}]\leq\frac{1+\sqrt{\epsilon}}{|F^{\prime}_{\rho}|},

\displaystyle\Tr[\hat{\tau}^{\hat{A}^{\prime}_{1}E}\hat{\hat{\tau}}^{\hat{A}^{\prime}_{1}E}]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{(1+3\sqrt{\epsilon})(\Tr\tau)^{2}\cdot 2^{-H_{\mathrm{min}}^{\epsilon}(A^{\prime}_{1}|E)_{\tau}}}{|F^{\prime}_{\tau}|},

\displaystyle\Tr[\hat{\rho}^{\hat{A}_{1}R}\hat{\hat{\rho}}^{\hat{A}_{1}R}]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{(1+3\sqrt{\epsilon})\cdot 2^{-H_{\mathrm{min}}^{\epsilon}(A_{1}|R)_{\rho}}}{|F^{\prime}_{\rho}|},

\displaystyle\Tr[\hat{\tau}^{\hat{A}^{\prime}_{2}E}\hat{\hat{\tau}}^{\hat{A}^{\prime}_{2}E}]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{(1+3\sqrt{\epsilon})(\Tr\tau)^{2}\cdot 2^{-H_{\mathrm{min}}^{\epsilon}(A^{\prime}_{2}|E)_{\tau}}}{|F^{\prime}_{\tau}|},

\displaystyle\Tr[\hat{\rho}^{\hat{A}_{2}R}\hat{\hat{\rho}}^{\hat{A}_{2}R}]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{(1+3\sqrt{\epsilon})\cdot 2^{-H_{\mathrm{min}}^{\epsilon}(A_{2}|R)_{\rho}}}{|F^{\prime}_{\rho}|},

\displaystyle\Tr[\hat{\tau}^{\hat{A}^{\prime}_{1}\hat{A}^{\prime}_{2}E}\hat{\hat{\tau}}^{\hat{A}^{\prime}_{1}\hat{A}^{\prime}_{2}E}]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{(1+3\sqrt{\epsilon})(\Tr\tau)^{2}\cdot 2^{-H_{\mathrm{min}}^{\epsilon}(A^{\prime}_{1}A^{\prime}_{2}|E)_{\tau}}}{|F^{\prime}_{\tau}|},

\displaystyle\Tr[\hat{\rho}^{\hat{A}_{1}\hat{A}_{2}R}\hat{\hat{\rho}}^{\hat{A}_{1}\hat{A}_{2}R}]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{(1+3\sqrt{\epsilon})\cdot 2^{-H_{\mathrm{min}}^{\epsilon}(A_{1}A_{2}|R)_{\rho}}}{|F^{\prime}_{\rho}|}.

Plugging Lemma
25
into
Equation
86
and using the constraints on the
entropic terms and Hilbert space dimensions given in the assumption
of the theorem, we get

\displaystyle\E_{U^{\hat{A}_{1}},U^{\hat{A}_{2}}}[\Tr[\hat{\sigma}(U^{\hat{A}_{1}},U^{\hat{A}_{2}})^{ER}\hat{\hat{\sigma}}(U^{\hat{A}_{1}},U^{\hat{A}_{2}})^{ER}]]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{|A_{1}|^{2}|A_{2}|^{2}}{(|A_{1}|^{2}-1)(|A_{2}|^{2}-1)}\cdot\frac{(1+\sqrt{\epsilon})^{2}(\Tr\tau)^{2}}{|F^{\prime}_{\tau}||F^{\prime}_{\rho}|}

\displaystyle{}+\frac{|A_{1}|^{2}|A_{2}|^{2}}{(|A_{1}|^{2}-1)(|A_{2}|^{2}-1)}\cdot\frac{(1+3\sqrt{\epsilon})^{2}(\Tr\tau)^{2}\cdot 2^{-H_{\mathrm{min}}^{\epsilon}(A^{\prime}_{1}|E)_{\tau}-H_{\mathrm{min}}^{\epsilon}(A_{1}|R)_{\rho}}}{|F^{\prime}_{\tau}||F^{\prime}_{\rho}|}

\displaystyle{}+\frac{|A_{1}|^{2}|A_{2}|^{2}}{(|A_{1}|^{2}-1)(|A_{2}|^{2}-1)}\cdot\frac{(1+3\sqrt{\epsilon})^{2}(\Tr\tau)^{2}\cdot 2^{-H_{\mathrm{min}}^{\epsilon}(A^{\prime}_{2}|E)_{\tau}-H_{\mathrm{min}}^{\epsilon}(A_{2}|R)_{\rho}}}{|F^{\prime}_{\tau}||F^{\prime}_{\rho}|}

\displaystyle{}+\frac{|A_{1}||A_{2}|}{(|A_{1}|^{2}-1)(|A_{2}|^{2}-1)}\cdot\frac{(1+\sqrt{\epsilon})(1+3\sqrt{\epsilon})(\Tr\tau)^{2}\cdot 2^{-H_{\mathrm{min}}^{\epsilon}(A_{1}A_{2}|R)_{\rho}}}{|F^{\prime}_{\tau}||F^{\prime}_{\rho}|}

\displaystyle{}+\frac{|A_{1}|^{2}|A_{2}|^{2}}{(|A_{1}|^{2}-1)(|A_{2}|^{2}-1)}\cdot\frac{(1+3\sqrt{\epsilon})^{2}(\Tr\tau)^{2}\cdot 2^{-H_{\mathrm{min}}^{\epsilon}(A^{\prime}_{1}A^{\prime}_{2}|E)_{\tau}-H_{\mathrm{min}}^{\epsilon}(A_{1}A_{2}|R)_{\rho}}}{|F^{\prime}_{\tau}||F^{\prime}_{\rho}|}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{|A_{1}|^{2}|A_{2}|^{2}}{(|A_{1}|^{2}-1)(|A_{2}|^{2}-1)}\cdot\frac{(1+3\sqrt{\epsilon})(\Tr\tau)^{2}}{|F^{\prime}_{\tau}||F^{\prime}_{\rho}|}

\displaystyle{}+\frac{|A_{1}|^{2}|A_{2}|^{2}}{(|A_{1}|^{2}-1)(|A_{2}|^{2}-1)}\cdot\frac{(1+9\sqrt{\epsilon})(\Tr\tau)^{2}\cdot 2^{-H_{\mathrm{min}}^{\epsilon}(A^{\prime}_{1}|E)_{\tau}-H_{\mathrm{min}}^{\epsilon}(A_{1}|R)_{\rho}}}{|F^{\prime}_{\tau}||F^{\prime}_{\rho}|}

\displaystyle{}+\frac{|A_{1}|^{2}|A_{2}|^{2}}{(|A_{1}|^{2}-1)(|A_{2}|^{2}-1)}\cdot\frac{(1+9\sqrt{\epsilon})(\Tr\tau)^{2}\cdot 2^{-H_{\mathrm{min}}^{\epsilon}(A^{\prime}_{2}|E)_{\tau}-H_{\mathrm{min}}^{\epsilon}(A_{2}|R)_{\rho}}}{|F^{\prime}_{\tau}||F^{\prime}_{\rho}|}

\displaystyle{}+\frac{|A_{1}|^{2}|A_{2}|^{2}}{(|A_{1}|^{2}-1)(|A_{2}|^{2}-1)}\cdot\frac{(1+7\sqrt{\epsilon})(\Tr\tau)^{2}\cdot 2^{-H_{\mathrm{min}}^{\epsilon}(A^{\prime}_{1}A^{\prime}_{2}|E)_{\tau}-H_{\mathrm{min}}^{\epsilon}(A_{1}A_{2}|R)_{\rho}}}{|F^{\prime}_{\tau}||F^{\prime}_{\rho}|}

\displaystyle{}+\frac{|A_{1}|^{2}|A_{2}|^{2}}{(|A_{1}|^{2}-1)(|A_{2}|^{2}-1)}\cdot\frac{(1+9\sqrt{\epsilon})(\Tr\tau)^{2}\cdot 2^{-H_{\mathrm{min}}^{\epsilon}(A^{\prime}_{1}A^{\prime}_{2}|E)_{\tau}-H_{\mathrm{min}}^{\epsilon}(A_{1}A_{2}|R)_{\rho}}}{|F^{\prime}_{\tau}||F^{\prime}_{\rho}|}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle(1-\epsilon)^{-2}\cdot\frac{(1+3\sqrt{\epsilon})(\Tr\tau)^{2}}{|F^{\prime}_{\tau}||F^{\prime}_{\rho}|}+(1-\epsilon)^{-2}\cdot\frac{\epsilon(1+9\sqrt{\epsilon})(\Tr\tau)^{2}}{|F^{\prime}_{\tau}||F^{\prime}_{\rho}|}

\displaystyle{}+(1-\epsilon)^{-2}\cdot\frac{\epsilon(1+9\sqrt{\epsilon})(\Tr\tau)^{2}}{|F^{\prime}_{\tau}||F^{\prime}_{\rho}|}+(1-\epsilon)^{-2}\cdot\frac{\epsilon(2+16\sqrt{\epsilon})(\Tr\tau)^{2}}{|F^{\prime}_{\tau}||F^{\prime}_{\rho}|}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{(1+9\sqrt{\epsilon})(\Tr\tau)^{2}}{|F^{\prime}_{\tau}||F^{\prime}_{\rho}|}+\frac{14\epsilon(\Tr\tau)^{2}}{|F^{\prime}_{\tau}||F^{\prime}_{\rho}|}<\frac{(1+23\sqrt{\epsilon})(\Tr\tau)^{2}}{|F^{\prime}_{\tau}||F^{\prime}_{\rho}|},

where we used Fact
4

in the second inequality above.
Combined with Equation
83
, Theorem
3

is finally proved.

9
Conclusion

In this paper, we have noted how the telescoping technique to prove
multipartite soft covering results fails
when pairwise
independence is lost amongst certain probability distributions involved
in soft covering. We have then developed a sophisticated machinery
that recovers the desired smooth inner bounds for multipartite soft
covering under
a slight loss of pairwise independence described precisely in
Definition
3
. To develop this machinery,
we had to combine existing results on tilting and augmentation smoothing
of quantum states from
[
Sen21b
]
together with a novel
observation that the naive classical strategy for flattening probability
distributions in fact preserves the fidelity of quantum states. We have
then seen how this machinery allows us to prove a new inner bound
for sending private classical information over a wiretap quantum
interference channel by combining existing inner bounds in the
non wiretap case from
[
Sen21a
]
together with our
smooth soft covering results. Such an inner bound in the non pairwise
independent case was unknown earlier even in the classical asymptotic
iid setting.

Our work leads to natural avenues for further research.
One direction to pursue is to further
extend our tilting, augmentation smoothing and flattening based
machinery, and find more applications to quantum Shannon theory.
In spirit, our machinery is arguably much closer to simultaneous
smoothing than telescoping.
It gives
a unified treatment of one shot multiparty smooth packing type
questions as in

[
Sen21b
,
Sen21a
,
DGHW20
]
, as well as one
one shot smooth multiparty
convex split and soft covering questions described in this paper. ‘
However, one important application where our machinery fails is in
proving a fully smooth multipartite expander matrix Chernoff bound.
The paper
[
Sen25
]
proves a fully smooth multipartite
classical quantum soft covering lemma in concentration, aka fully
smooth multipartite matrix Chernoff bound, but for independent choices
of samples by each classical party. That paper also proves a smooth
unipartite soft covering lemma in concentration when the samples
are taken by the single classical party from a random walk on an
expander graph, which can be called a unipartite expander matrix Chernoff
bound. However, the techniques of that paper
fail to prove a multipartite expander matrix Chernoff bound.
Expander walks are a key example where pairwise independence amongst the
classical samples of a party is lost. Unfortunately the loss of pairwise
independence does not fit the framework behind
Definition
3
. Hence even fully smooth
multipartite classical quantum soft covering in expectation under
expander walks cannot be handled by the techniques of the present paper.
Proving fully smooth multipartite soft covering results in expectation
and concentration for expander walks is thus a very important open problem.

It is also important to find limitations of our tilting and
augmentation smoothing based machinery. One
immediate limitation that comes to mind is that
augmenting quantum states requires tensoring with fresh completely
mixed ancilla states. This is required
so that tracing out a set of registers essentially removes the tilts
along the traced out directions when quantified in terms of
the Schatten-
\ell_{\infty}
norm. Augmentation can be done without
loss of generality for the packing type questions studied in earlier
works, as well as in convex split and soft covering problems. However,
high dimensional augmentation becomes a bottleneck for decoupling
problems. This is why we gave the simpler proof for fully smooth
convex split
in Section
7
which did not need augmentation for
smoothing purposes.
The proof in Section
7
only had to enlarge the
dimensions of the
Hilbert spaces
X
and
Y
by factor of
2
in order to achieve the
correct tilting. It led to the proof of
a fully smooth decoupling theorem in Section
8
.
Though the decoupling theorem in
Section
8
suffices for all the applications of
decoupling in quantum
information theory that we know of, the factor of
2
blowup is still
mathematically unsatisfactory. In contrast, the telescoping based proof
of fully smooth decoupling in
[
Sen24
]
has no such blowup.
Removing this factor of
2

for decoupling for our machinery is an interesting open problem.
